                if Nactive <= allocated_indices:
                    break
                elif reallocated:
                    msg = f"Block index buffer is still too small after reallocation ({allocated_indices} < {Nactive})."
                    logger.error(msg)
                    raise RuntimeError(msg)

                if output_is_ping:
                    self.block_index_ping_buffer = Nactive
                else:
                    self.block_index_pong_buffer = Nactive

            if time_kernel:
                elapsed += tk

            if Nactive == 0:
                break

            current_active_blocks = Nactive

        assert Nactive == 0 or output_is_ping, (Nactive, output_is_ping)
        return Nactive, elapsed

    def _autotune_kernels(self, stream, device, params):
        logger = self.logger

        logger.info(f"Autotuning kernels with new configuration by using autotuning rigor {self.autotuning_rigor}.")

        Ts = self._generate_candidate_thread_size(device)
        Bs = self._generate_candidate_block_size(device)

        # Heuristics to get best sizes without measuring:
        #   1) use smallest block with predicate, else second biggest one
        #   2) use third highest thread size for thread based kernels
        B0 = Bs[-1 if self.has_block_predicate else 1 % len(Bs)]
        T0 = Ts[min(2, len(Ts) - 1)]
        use_cmem0 = False if self.dim == 3 else None

        rigor = self.autotuning_rigor
        if rigor == AutotuningRigor.ESTIMATE:
            return (B0, T0, use_cmem0)
        elif rigor == AutotuningRigor.MEASURE:
            Nruns = 1
            max_candidates = 4
        elif rigor == AutotuningRigor.PATIENT:
            Nruns = 1
            max_candidates = 16
        elif rigor == AutotuningRigor.EXHAUSTIVE:
            Nruns = 1
            max_candidates = 64
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError(rigor)

        logger.debug(f"Autotuning parameters: max_candidates={max_candidates}, nruns={Nruns}")

        self._allocate_scalar_field()

        try:
            Bopt, use_cmem = self._autotune_block_size(stream, device, params, T0, Nruns, max_candidates)
            self._reset_allocated_buffers()

            Topt = self._autotune_thread_size(stream, device, params, Bopt, use_cmem, Nruns, max_candidates)
            self._reset_allocated_buffers()
        except _NoActiveBlocksError:
            logger.warning(
                f"Cannot autotune kernels with rigor {rigor} because there is no active block "
                f"for current parameters, falling back to {AutotuningRigor.ESTIMATE}."
            )
            self._reset_allocated_buffers()
            return (B0, T0, use_cmem0)

        return (Bopt, Topt, use_cmem)

    def _autotune_block_size(self, stream, device, params, Nthreads, Nruns, max_candidates):
        dim = self.dim
        N = self.N
        itype = self.itype
        ftype = self.ftype
        backend = self.backend
        logger = self.logger

        Bs = self._generate_candidate_block_size(device)

        # use smaller block size first with predicate
        if self.has_block_predicate:
            Bs = Bs[::-1]

        logger.debug(f"Estimating block size for N={N}, candidates are:")
        logger.debug("  " + ", ".join(map(_tuple_str, Bs)))

        warp_size = device.warp_size

        results = {}

        ncandidates = 0
        allocation_error = False

        use_cmem_choices = (True, False) if dim == 3 else (None,)

        for use_cmem in use_cmem_choices:
            if ncandidates >= max_candidates or allocation_error:
                break

            for B in Bs:
                if allocation_error:
                    logger.warning("Device allocation error, ignoring other kernel candidates.")
                    break

                if ncandidates >= max_candidates:
                    logger.debug(f"Max kernel candidates achieved ({max_candidates}).")
                    break

                threads_per_blocks = np.prod(B, dtype=np.int64)
                G = (N + B - 2) // (B - 1)
                Nblocks = np.prod(G, dtype=itype)
                Ngrid = itype(Nblocks + Nthreads - 1) // Nthreads
                shared_memory_bytes = self._required_shared_memory_bytes(
                    dim, warp_size, threads_per_blocks, ftype, itype
                )

                logger.debug(
                    f"Benchmarking with B={B}, G={G}, use_cmem={'1' if use_cmem else '0'}, "
                    f"shared_mem={bytes2str(shared_memory_bytes)}..."
                )

                try:
                    kernels = self._build_kernels(device, B, Nthreads, use_cmem=use_cmem)
                except CudaBlockSizeError as e:
                    logger.debug(f"  skipping candidate B={_tuple_str(B)}: {e}")
                    continue

                try:
                    self.counting_buffer = 2

                    if self.has_block_predicate:
                        levels, splits = self._determine_multiscale_parameters(
                            G,
                            device.multiprocessor_count * device.max_threads_per_multiprocessor,
                            dim,
                        )

                        elapsed0_time_s = []
                        for i in range(Nruns):
                            Nactive, tk = self._compact_active_blocks_from_predicate(
                                kernels["compact_active_blocks_from_predicate"],
                                stream,
                                Nthreads,
                                levels,
                                splits,
                                params,
                                time_kernel=True,
                            )
                            elapsed0_time_s.append(tk)
                        elapsed0_time_s = np.asarray(elapsed0_time_s, dtype=np.float64)
                        logger.debug(
                            f"  compact_active_blocks_from_predicate: nruns={Nruns}, "
                            f"mean={time2str(elapsed0_time_s.mean())}, "
                            f"min={time2str(elapsed0_time_s.min())}, "
                            f"max={time2str(elapsed0_time_s.max())}"
                        )
                    else:
                        self.block_mask_buffer = Nblocks

                        elapsed0_time_s = []
                        for i in range(Nruns):
                            elapsed0_time_s.append(
                                kernels["compute_block_mask"](
                                    tuple(G),
                                    tuple(B),
                                    self._compute_block_mask_kernel_args(params),
                                    stream=stream,
                                    time_kernel=True,
                                )
                            )
                        elapsed0_time_s = np.asarray(elapsed0_time_s, dtype=np.float64)

                        self._reset_counters(stream)
                        kernels["count_active_blocks"](
                            (Ngrid,),
                            (Nthreads,),
                            self._count_active_blocks_kernel_args(Nblocks),
                            stream=stream,
                        )
                        Nactive = int(backend.asnumpy(self.counting_buffer[:2], stream)[0])

                        self.block_index_ping_buffer = Nactive

                        self._reset_counters(stream)
                        kernels["compact_active_blocks"](
                            (Ngrid,),
                            (Nthreads,),
                            self._compact_active_blocks_kernel_args(Nblocks),
                            stream=stream,
                        )
                        Nactive_bis = int(backend.asnumpy(self.counting_buffer[:2], stream)[0])
                        assert Nactive == Nactive_bis, (Nactive, Nactive_bis)

                    if Nactive == 0:
                        raise _NoActiveBlocksError("Cannot autotune if there is no active blocks.")

                    self._reset_counters(stream)
                    kernels["compute_simplices"](
                        (Nactive,),
                        tuple(B),
                        self._compute_simplices_kernel_args(params),
                        shared_mem=shared_memory_bytes,
                        stream=stream,
                    )
                    Ncount = backend.asnumpy(self.counting_buffer[:2], stream)

                    self.vertex_buffer = Ncount[0]
                    self.simplex_buffer = Ncount[1]
                except backend.AllocationError:
                    allocation_error = True
                    continue

                elapsed1_time_s = []
                for i in range(Nruns):
                    self._reset_counters(stream)
                    elapsed1_time_s.append(
                        kernels["compute_simplices"](
                            (Nactive,),
                            tuple(B),
                            self._compute_simplices_kernel_args(params),
                            shared_mem=shared_memory_bytes,
                            time_kernel=True,
                            stream=stream,
                        )
                    )
                elapsed1_time_s = np.asarray(elapsed1_time_s, dtype=np.float64)
                logger.debug(
                    f"  compute_simplices: nruns={Nruns}, "
                    f"mean={time2str(elapsed1_time_s.mean())}, "
                    f"min={time2str(elapsed1_time_s.min())}, "
                    f"max={time2str(elapsed1_time_s.max())}"
                )
                Ncount_bis = backend.asnumpy(self.counting_buffer[:2], stream)
                assert (Ncount == Ncount_bis).all(), (Ncount, Ncount_bis)

                elapsed = elapsed0_time_s + elapsed1_time_s
                results[(tuple(B), use_cmem)] = elapsed.mean()
                ncandidates += 1

        if ncandidates == 0:
            msg = "Could not benchmark any kernel, cannot continue."
            logger.error(msg)
            raise RuntimeError(msg)

        logger.info(f"Successfully benchmarked {ncandidates} kernel candidates to determine block size:")
        blength = max(len(_tuple_str(b)) for b, _ in results.keys())
        for (B, cmem), v in sorted(results.items(), key=operator.itemgetter(1)):
            logger.info(f"  B={_tuple_str(B):{blength}s}, use_cmem={'1' if cmem else '0'}: {time2str(v)}")

        (Bopt, use_cmem), _ = next(iter(sorted(results.items(), key=operator.itemgetter(1))))
        logger.info(f"Best candidate block size is {_tuple_str(Bopt)} with use_cmem={'1' if use_cmem else '0'}.")
        return (np.asarray(Bopt, dtype=itype), use_cmem)

    def _autotune_thread_size(self, stream, device, params, B, use_cmem, Nruns, max_candidates):
        backend = self.backend
        N = self.N
        dim = self.dim
        itype = self.itype
        logger = self.logger

        Ts = self._generate_candidate_thread_size(device)

        logger.debug(f"Estimating thread size for N={N}, candidates are:")
        logger.debug("  " + ", ".join(map(str, Ts)))

        G = (N + B - 2) // (B - 1)
        Nblocks = np.prod(G, dtype=itype)

        results = {}
        ncandidates = 0
        allocation_error = False
        for Nthreads in Ts:
            if allocation_error:
                logger.warning("Device allocation error, ignoring other kernel candidates.")
                break

            if ncandidates >= max_candidates:
                logger.debug(f"Max kernel candidates achieved ({max_candidates}).")
                break

            logger.debug(f"Benchmarking with Nthreads={Nthreads}...")

            try:
                kernels = self._build_kernels(device, B, Nthreads, use_cmem)
            except CudaBlockSizeError as e:
                logger.debug(f"  skipping candidate Nthreads={Nthreads}: {e}")
                continue

            try:
                self.counting_buffer = 2

                if self.has_block_predicate:
                    levels, splits = self._determine_multiscale_parameters(
                        G,
                        device.multiprocessor_count * device.max_threads_per_multiprocessor,
                        dim,
                    )

                    elapsed0_time_s = []
                    for i in range(Nruns):
                        Nactive, tk = self._compact_active_blocks_from_predicate(
                            kernels["compact_active_blocks_from_predicate"],
                            stream,
                            Nthreads,
                            levels,
                            splits,
                            params,
                            time_kernel=True,
                        )
                        elapsed0_time_s.append(tk)
                    elapsed0_time_s = np.asarray(elapsed0_time_s, dtype=np.float64)
                    logger.debug(
                        f"  compact_active_blocks_from_predicate: nruns={Nruns}, "
                        f"mean={time2str(elapsed0_time_s.mean())}, "
                        f"min={time2str(elapsed0_time_s.min())}, "
                        f"max={time2str(elapsed0_time_s.max())}"
                    )
                    elapsed = elapsed0_time_s.mean()
                else:
                    Ngrid = itype(Nblocks + Nthreads - 1) // Nthreads
                    self.block_mask_buffer = Nblocks

                    kernels["compute_block_mask"](
                        tuple(G),
                        tuple(B),
                        self._compute_block_mask_kernel_args(params),
                        stream=stream,
                    )

                    elapsed0_time_s = []
                    for i in range(Nruns):
                        self._reset_counters(stream)
                        elapsed0_time_s.append(
                            kernels["count_active_blocks"](
                                (Ngrid,),
                                (Nthreads,),
                                self._count_active_blocks_kernel_args(Nblocks),
                                time_kernel=True,
                                stream=stream,
                            )
                        )
                    elapsed0_time_s = np.asarray(elapsed0_time_s, dtype=np.float64)
                    logger.debug(
                        f"  count_active_blocks: nruns={Nruns}, "
                        f"mean={time2str(elapsed0_time_s.mean())}, "
                        f"min={time2str(elapsed0_time_s.min())}, "
                        f"max={time2str(elapsed0_time_s.max())}"
                    )
                    Nactive = int(backend.asnumpy(self.counting_buffer[:2], stream)[0])

                    self.block_index_ping_buffer = Nactive

                    elapsed1_time_s = []
                    for i in range(Nruns):
                        self._reset_counters(stream)
                        elapsed1_time_s.append(
                            kernels["compact_active_blocks"](
                                (Ngrid,),
                                (Nthreads,),
                                self._compact_active_blocks_kernel_args(Nblocks),
                                time_kernel=True,
                                stream=stream,
                            )
                        )
                    elapsed1_time_s = np.asarray(elapsed1_time_s, dtype=np.float64)
                    logger.debug(
                        f"  compact_active_blocks: nruns={Nruns}, "
                        f"mean={time2str(elapsed1_time_s.mean())}, "
                        f"min={time2str(elapsed1_time_s.min())}, "
                        f"max={time2str(elapsed1_time_s.max())}"
                    )
                    Nactive_bis = int(backend.asnumpy(self.counting_buffer[:2], stream)[0])
                    assert Nactive == Nactive_bis, (Nactive, Nactive_bis)
                    elapsed = elapsed0_time_s.mean() + elapsed1_time_s.mean()
            except backend.AllocationError:
                allocation_error = True
                continue

            if Nactive == 0:
                raise _NoActiveBlocksError("Cannot autotune if there is no active blocks.")

            results[Nthreads] = elapsed
            ncandidates += 1

        if not results:
            msg = "Could not benchmark any kernel."
            logger.error(msg)
            raise RuntimeError(msg)

        logger.info(f"Successfully benchmarked {ncandidates} kernel candidates to determine thread size:")
        for T, v in sorted(results.items(), key=operator.itemgetter(1)):
            logger.info(f"  T={int(T):4d}: {time2str(v)}")

        Topt, _ = next(iter(sorted(results.items(), key=operator.itemgetter(1))))
        logger.info(f"Best candidate thread size is {Topt}.")
        return Topt

    def _determine_multiscale_parameters(self, G, initial_domain_count, levels_per_round):
        dim = G.size

        end_level = np.ceil(np.log2(G)).astype(np.int32)
        end_levels = end_level.sum()

        start_levels = min(end_levels, int(np.floor(np.log2(initial_domain_count))))

        Nlevels = end_levels - start_levels
        Nrounds = (Nlevels + levels_per_round - 1) // levels_per_round

        start_level = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.int32)
        while start_level.sum() != start_levels:
            block_size = self.L / (2**start_level)
            can_update = set(np.where(start_level < end_level)[0])
            for k in np.argsort(-block_size, kind="stable"):
                if k in can_update:
                    start_level[k] += 1
                    break
            else:
                raise RuntimeError

        level = start_level.copy()
        splits = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.int32)
        levels = np.zeros(
            (
                Nrounds + 2,
                dim,
            ),
            dtype=np.int32,
        )
        levels[0] = level
        levels[1] = level
        l = 2
        while (level != end_level).any():
            block_size = self.L / (2**level)
            can_update = set(np.where(level < end_level)[0])
            for k in np.argsort(-block_size, kind="stable"):
                if k in can_update:
                    level[k] += 1
                    splits[k] += 1
                    if splits.sum() == levels_per_round:
                        levels[l] = level
                        splits[...] = 0
                        l += 1
                    break
            else:
                raise RuntimeError
        if any(splits):
            levels[l] = level

        splits = np.diff(levels, axis=0)

        return levels, splits

    def _build_kernel_launcher(self, device, B, Nthreads, use_cmem):
        dim = self.dim
        N = self.N
        itype = self.itype
        ftype = self.ftype

        self.logger.info(
            f"Building kernel launcher for N={N}, B={B}, Nthreads={Nthreads}"
            + ("" if use_cmem is None else f", use_cmem={'1' if use_cmem else '0'}...")
        )

        G = (N + B - 2) // (B - 1)
        Nblocks = np.prod(G, dtype=itype)
        Ngrid = itype(Nblocks + Nthreads - 1) // Nthreads

        if self.has_block_predicate:
            max_resident_threads = device.multiprocessor_count * device.max_threads_per_multiprocessor
            levels, splits = self._determine_multiscale_parameters(
                G,
                max_resident_threads,
                dim,
            )
        else:
            levels, splits = None, None

        warp_size = device.warp_size
        threads_per_blocks = np.prod(B, dtype=np.int64)

        shared_mem = self._required_shared_memory_bytes(dim, warp_size, threads_per_blocks, ftype, itype)

        kernels = self._build_kernels(device, B, Nthreads, use_cmem)

        def kernel_launcher(
            stream,
            params,
            to_numpy,
            return_elapsed_time,
            s=self,
            kernels=kernels,
            shared_mem=shared_mem,
            G=tuple(G),
            B=tuple(B),
            Nthreads=(Nthreads,),
            Ngrid=(Ngrid,),
            Nblocks=Nblocks,
            levels=levels,
            splits=splits,
            counter_views=[None, None],
        ):
            tstart = time.perf_counter()

            backend = s.backend
            logger = s.logger
            debug = logger.isEnabledFor(logging.DEBUG)

            if debug:
                pretty_params = ", ".join(f"{pname}={pvalue}" for (pname, pvalue) in zip(s.extra_params.keys(), params))
                logger.debug(
                    f"Kernel launcher called with parameters {pretty_params}."
                    if pretty_params
                    else "Kernel launcher called."
                )

            def get_scalar_field_buffer(x):
                if not s.export_scalar_field:
                    return tuple()
                x = x[: s.Nscalars]
                if to_numpy:
                    x = backend.asnumpy(x, stream)
                # scalar field is stored x-fastest: S[(iz,)iy,ix] (C-contiguous, shape N[::-1]+1)
                x = x.reshape(tuple(map(int, s.N[::-1] + 1)))
                return (x,)

            def finalize(buffers):
                backend.synchronize_stream(stream)
                elapsed = time.perf_counter() - tstart
                if debug:
                    logger.debug(f"Kernel launcher finished in {time2str(elapsed)}.")
                if return_elapsed_time:
                    return buffers, elapsed
                else:
                    return buffers

            def empty_result():
                buffers = (None, None)
                buffers += get_scalar_field_buffer(s.scalar_field_buffer)
                buffers += (None,) * len(s.vertex_properties)
                return finalize(buffers)

            def launch_step_by_step():
                """
                Launch the kernels step by step, counts are read back after each step to allocate the buffers.
                Returns the vertex and simplex counts, or None when there is no active block.
                """
                s.counting_buffer = 2

                if s.has_block_predicate:
                    if debug:
                        logger.debug(
                            f"Compacting {np.prod(G, dtype=np.uint64)} blocks by using predicate, "
                            f"target block grid size is {G}."
                        )
                    Nactive, _ = s._compact_active_blocks_from_predicate(
                        kernels["compact_active_blocks_from_predicate"],
                        stream,
                        Nthreads[0],
                        levels,
                        splits,
                        params,
                    )
                    if Nactive == 0:
                        logger.debug("No active blocks found by predicate.")
                        return None
                else:
                    s.block_mask_buffer = Nblocks
                    s._allocate_scalar_field()

                    if debug:
                        logger.debug(f"Computing block mask for {Nblocks} blocks.")
                    kernels["compute_block_mask"](
                        G,
                        B,
                        s._compute_block_mask_kernel_args(params),
                        stream=stream,
                    )

                    is_first_call = s.allocated_ping_block_index_count == 0
                    if is_first_call:
                        logger.debug("Counting active blocks because allocated block index size is zero.")
                        s._reset_counters(stream)
                        kernels["count_active_blocks"](
                            Ngrid,
                            Nthreads,
                            s._count_active_blocks_kernel_args(Nblocks),
                            stream=stream,
                        )
                    else:
                        if debug:
                            logger.debug(
                                "Compacting active blocks "
                                f"(allocated_block_index={s.allocated_ping_block_index_count})."
                            )
                        s._reset_counters(stream)
                        kernels["compact_active_blocks"](
                            Ngrid,
                            Nthreads,
                            s._compact_active_blocks_kernel_args(Nblocks),
                            stream=stream,
                        )

                    Nactive = int(backend.asnumpy(s.counting_buffer[:2], stream)[0])
                    if Nactive == 0:
                        logger.debug("No active blocks found, skipping block compaction.")
                        return None

                    if is_first_call or (s.allocated_ping_block_index_count < Nactive):
                        logger.info(f"Reallocating block index buffer to handle {Nactive} active blocks.")
                        s.block_index_ping_buffer = Nactive

                        logger.debug("Compacting active blocks with reallocated buffer.")
                        s._reset_counters(stream)
                        kernels["compact_active_blocks"](
                            Ngrid,
                            Nthreads,
                            s._compact_active_blocks_kernel_args(Nblocks),
                            stream=stream,
                        )

                if debug:
                    logger.debug(
                        "Generating simplices "
                        f"(allocated_vertices={s.allocated_vertex_count}, "
                        f"allocated_indices={s.allocated_simplices_count})"
                    )
                s._reset_counters(stream)
                kernels["compute_simplices"](
                    (Nactive,),
                    B,
                    s._compute_simplices_kernel_args(params),
                    shared_mem=shared_mem,
                    stream=stream,
                )

                Nvertices, Nsimplices = map(int, backend.asnumpy(s.counting_buffer[:2], stream))
                reallocate_vertices = s.allocated_vertex_count < Nvertices
                reallocate_indices = s.allocated_simplices_count < Nsimplices

                if debug:
                    fmt = functools.partial(decimal_unit2str, unit="")
                    logger.debug(
                        f"Generated {fmt(Nvertices)} vertices ({fmt(s.allocated_vertex_count)} allocated), "
                        + ("reallocation is required " if reallocate_vertices else "vertex buffer was large enough ")
                        + (
                            f"({100.0*Nvertices/s.allocated_vertex_count:2.1f}% filling)."
                            if s.allocated_vertex_count
                            else "(buffer was not allocated)."
                        )
                    )
                    logger.debug(
                        f"Generated {fmt(Nsimplices)} simplices ({fmt(s.allocated_simplices_count)} allocated), "
                        + ("reallocation is required " if reallocate_indices else "index buffer was large enough ")
                        + (
                            f"({100.0*Nsimplices/s.allocated_simplices_count:2.1f}% filling)."
                            if s.allocated_simplices_count
                            else "(buffer was not allocated)."
                        )
                    )

                if reallocate_vertices:
                    logger.info(f"Reallocating vertex buffer to handle {Nvertices} vertices.")
                    s.vertex_buffer = Nvertices

                if reallocate_indices:
                    logger.info(f"Reallocating simplex buffer to handle {Nsimplices} indices.")
                    s.simplex_buffer = Nsimplices

                if reallocate_vertices or reallocate_indices:
                    logger.debug("Generating simplices with reallocated buffers.")
                    s._reset_counters(stream)
                    kernels["compute_simplices"](
                        (Nactive,),
                        B,
                        s._compute_simplices_kernel_args(params),
                        shared_mem=shared_mem,
                        stream=stream,
                    )

                if s.has_vertex_properties and (Nvertices > 0):
                    for vp in s.vertex_properties:
                        s._update_cuda_buffer(vp, Nvertices)

                    logger.debug("Computing vertex properties.")
                    kernels["compute_vertex_properties"](
                        ((Nvertices + Nthreads[0] - 1) // Nthreads[0],),
                        Nthreads,
                        s._compute_vertex_properties_kernel_args(params, Nvertices),
                        stream=stream,
                    )

                return Nvertices, Nsimplices

            def launch_device_driven():
                """
                Launch all the kernels without intermediate host synchronizations: kernels read their number of
                input blocks (or vertices) from device counters and grids are sized from the buffer capacities.
                Returns the vertex and simplex counts after a single device to host copy, or None when a buffer is
                not allocated yet or was too small (kernels are then launched again step by step).
                """
                vertex_capacity = s.allocated_vertex_count
                if s.has_vertex_properties:
                    vertex_capacity = min([vertex_capacity] + [s.allocated_elements(vp) for vp in s.vertex_properties])
                simplex_capacity = s.allocated_simplices_count
                ping_capacity = s.allocated_ping_block_index_count
                nsteps = splits.shape[0] if s.has_block_predicate else 1
                pong_capacity = s.allocated_pong_block_index_count if (nsteps > 1) else ping_capacity
                if min(ping_capacity, pong_capacity, vertex_capacity, simplex_capacity) == 0:
                    return None

                # counters: active blocks after each step, vertices and simplices (views are reused between calls)
                s.counting_buffer = nsteps + 2
                counters = s.counting_buffer
                if counter_views[0] is not counters:
                    counter_views[:] = [
                        counters,
                        (
                            tuple(counters[k : k + 1] for k in range(nsteps)),
                            counters[nsteps : nsteps + 1],
                            counters[nsteps : nsteps + 2],
                            counters[: nsteps + 2],
                        ),
                    ]
                active_counts, vertex_count, output_counts, all_counts = counter_views[1]
                s._reset_counters(stream)

                block_capacities = []
                if s.has_block_predicate:
                    max_blocks = 1 << int(levels[0].sum())
                    for k in range(nsteps):
                        args, output_is_ping = s._compact_active_blocks_from_predicate_kernel_args(
                            k,
                            max_blocks,
                            levels,
                            splits,
                            params,
                            input_count=(None if (k == 0) else active_counts[k - 1]),
                            output_count=active_counts[k],
                        )
                        kernels["compact_active_blocks_from_predicate"](
                            ((max_blocks + Nthreads[0] - 1) // Nthreads[0],),
                            Nthreads,
                            args,
                            stream=stream,
                        )
                        capacity = ping_capacity if output_is_ping else pong_capacity
                        block_capacities.append(capacity)
                        max_blocks = min(capacity, 1 << int(levels[k + 1].sum()))
                else:
                    s.block_mask_buffer = Nblocks
                    s._allocate_scalar_field()
                    kernels["compute_block_mask"](
                        G,
                        B,
                        s._compute_block_mask_kernel_args(params),
                        stream=stream,
                    )
                    kernels["compact_active_blocks"](
                        Ngrid,
                        Nthreads,
                        s._compact_active_blocks_kernel_args(Nblocks, output_count=active_counts[0]),
                        stream=stream,
                    )
                    block_capacities.append(ping_capacity)
                    max_blocks = min(ping_capacity, int(Nblocks))

                kernels["compute_simplices"](
                    (max_blocks,),
                    B,
                    s._compute_simplices_kernel_args(
                        params,
                        active_count=active_counts[-1],
                        output_counts=output_counts,
                    ),
                    shared_mem=shared_mem,
                    stream=stream,
                )

                if s.has_vertex_properties:
                    kernels["compute_vertex_properties"](
                        ((vertex_capacity + Nthreads[0] - 1) // Nthreads[0],),
                        Nthreads,
                        s._compute_vertex_properties_kernel_args(params, vertex_capacity, vertex_count=vertex_count),
                        stream=stream,
                    )

                counts = tuple(map(int, backend.asnumpy(all_counts, stream)))
                Nactive, (Nvertices, Nsimplices) = counts[:nsteps], counts[nsteps:]
                too_small = (
                    any(n > c for (n, c) in zip(Nactive, block_capacities))
                    or (Nvertices > vertex_capacity)
                    or (Nsimplices > simplex_capacity)
                )
                if debug:
                    logger.debug(
                        f"Device driven launch generated {Nvertices} vertices and {Nsimplices} simplices "
                        f"from {Nactive[-1]} active blocks" + (", buffers were too small." if too_small else ".")
                    )
                return None if too_small else (Nvertices, Nsimplices)

            counts = launch_device_driven()
            if counts is None:
                counts = launch_step_by_step()
                if counts is None:
                    return empty_result()
            Nvertices, Nsimplices = counts

            def get_vertex_buffer(x):
                if Nvertices == 0:
                    return None
                x = x[:Nvertices]
                if to_numpy:
                    return backend.asnumpy(x, stream)
                return x

            def get_simplex_buffer(x):
                if Nsimplices == 0:
                    return None
                x = x[:Nsimplices]
                if to_numpy:
                    return backend.asnumpy(x, stream)
                return x

            buffers = (
                get_vertex_buffer(s.vertex_buffer),
                get_simplex_buffer(s.simplex_buffer),
            )
            buffers += get_scalar_field_buffer(s.scalar_field_buffer)
            buffers += tuple(map(get_vertex_buffer, s.vertex_properties_buffers))

            return finalize(buffers)

        self._kernel_launcher = kernel_launcher

    def ctype_to_dtype(self, ctype):
        if not isinstance(ctype, str):
            return normalize_scalar_type(ctype)
        ctype = ctype.strip()
        if ctype.startswith("const "):
            ctype = ctype[len("const ") :].strip()
        if ctype == "T":
            dtype = self.ftype
        elif ctype == "I":
            dtype = self.itype
        elif ctype in ctype_to_dtype:
            dtype = ctype_to_dtype[ctype]
        else:
            raise KeyError(ctype)
        return dtype
```

### cupmb/enums.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
import enum


class Enum(enum.Enum):
    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return self.name


class Backend(enum.Enum):
    CUPY = 0
    PYCUDA = 1


class BufferType(Enum):
    COUNTERS = 0
    SCALAR_FIELD = 1
    BLOCK_MASK = 2
    BLOCK_INDICES_PING = 3
    BLOCK_INDICES_PONG = 4
    VERTICES = 5
    SIMPLICES = 6


class AllocationPolicy(Enum):
    LOCK_BUFFER = 0
    NO_EXTRA_ALLOCATION = 1
    ALLOCATE_EXACT_SIZE = 2
    ALLOCATE_UPPER_POW2 = 3


class AutotuningRigor(enum.IntEnum):
    """Autotuning rigor, ordered from the cheapest to the most expensive (comparable)."""

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return self.name

    ESTIMATE = 0
    MEASURE = 1
    PATIENT = 2
    EXHAUSTIVE = 3
```

### cupmb/parameters.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#


class ExtraParameter:
    def __init__(self, ctype, update=None):
        if update is not None:
            assert callable(update), type(update)
        self.ctype = ctype
        self.update = update

    def __eq__(self, other):
        if not isinstance(other, ExtraParameter):
            return NotImplemented
        return (self.ctype == other.ctype) and (self.update == other.update)

    def __hash__(self):
        return hash((self.ctype, self.update))

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"ExtraParameter(ctype={self.ctype!r}, update={self.update!r})"

    @property
    def is_callable(self):
        return callable(self.update)

    def __call__(self, *args, **kwds):
        assert self.is_callable, "ExtraParameter is not callable."
        return self.update(*args, **kwds)
```

### cupmb/properties.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
import jinja2
import numpy as np

from cupmb.utils.types import dtype_to_ctype


class VertexProperty:
    _kernel_template = jinja2.Template("""
#include <trove/block.h>

namespace cupmb {

template <typename T, typename I, I dim, typename... Args>
__global__ void compute_vertex_properties(
    const I max_vertices,
    const I* const __restrict nvertices_ptr,
    const T* const __restrict vertices,
{%- for vp in vertex_properties %}
    {{vp.ctype(T,I)}}* const __restrict vertex_property_{{loop.index}},
{%- endfor %}
    const Args... fargs)
{
    const I global_index = threadIdx.x + blockIdx.x * blockDim.x;
    const I nvertices = min(__ldg(nvertices_ptr), max_vertices);

    for(I index = global_index; index < nvertices; index += gridDim.x * blockDim.x) {
        if (trove::warp_converged()) {
            const trove::array<T,dim> vertex = trove::load_array_warp_contiguous<dim>(vertices, index);
{%- for vp in vertex_properties %}
            trove::store_array_warp_contiguous<{{vp.size(dim)}}>(vertex_property_{{loop.index}},
                index, {{vp.fname}}<T,I,dim>(vertex, fargs...));
{%- endfor %}
        } else {
            const trove::array<T,dim> vertex = trove::load_array<dim>(vertices, index);
{%- for vp in vertex_properties %}
            trove::store_array<{{vp.size(dim)}}>(vertex_property_{{loop.index}},
                index, {{vp.fname}}<T,I,dim>(vertex, fargs...));
{%- endfor %}
        }
    }
}

}
""")

    @classmethod
    def generate_kernel(cls, T, I, dim, vertex_properties):
        assert T in {"float", "double"}, T
        assert I in {"unsigned int", "unsigned long long"}, I
        assert isinstance(dim, int) and dim >= 1, dim
        assert isinstance(vertex_properties, tuple), type(vertex_properties)
        assert all(map(lambda vp: isinstance(vp, cls), vertex_properties)), cls

        if len(vertex_properties) > 0:
            kernel_src = cls._kernel_template.render(T=T, I=I, dim=dim, vertex_properties=vertex_properties)
        else:
            kernel_src = ""

        return kernel_src

    def __init__(self, name, fname, dtype, shape):
        assert isinstance(name, str), type(name)
        assert isinstance(fname, str), type(fname)
        if not isinstance(dtype, str):
            # accept numpy scalar types, np.dtype instances and dtype-likes
            dtype = np.dtype(dtype).type
        assert isinstance(dtype, str) or issubclass(dtype, np.number), dtype
        assert len(name) > 0, name
        assert len(fname) > 0, fname

        if shape is None:
            shape = ()
        assert isinstance(shape, tuple), type(shape)

        self._name = name
        self._fname = fname
        self._dtype = dtype
        self._shape = shape

    def __str__(self):
        return self.name.upper()

    def ctype(self, T, I):
        dtype = self.dtype
        if dtype == "T":
            return T
        elif dtype == "I":
            return I
        elif isinstance(dtype, str):
            return dtype
        elif issubclass(dtype, np.number):
            return dtype_to_ctype[dtype]
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError(type(dtype))

    def effective_shape(self, dim):
        shape = []
        for s in self.shape:
            if isinstance(s, str):
                if s == "dim":
                    s = dim
                else:
                    raise NotImplementedError(s)
            shape.append(s)
        return tuple(shape)

    def size(self, dim):
        return np.prod(self.effective_shape(dim), dtype=np.int64)

    @property
    def name(self):
        return self._name

    @property
    def fname(self):
        return "cupmb::" + self._fname

    @property
    def dtype(self):
        return self._dtype

    @property
    def shape(self):
        return self._shape


if __name__ == "__main__":
    p0 = VertexProperty("N", fname="compute_normal", dtype="T", shape=("dim",))
    p1 = VertexProperty("P", fname="compute_property", dtype=np.uint8, shape=("dim", 4, "dim"))

    kernel = VertexProperty.generate_kernel(T="float", I="unsigned int", dim=3, vertex_properties=(p0, p1))
    print(kernel)
```

### examples/00_simple.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
import os, sys
import trimesh

if "CUPMB_LOGLEVEL" not in os.environ:
    os.environ["CUPMB_LOGLEVEL"] = "DEBUG"

from cupmb import ParallelMarchingBlocks, Backend, logger

if __name__ == "__main__":

    fdef = r"""
namespace cupmb {
    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ T scalar(
            const trove::array<T,dim>& p,
            const trove::array<I,dim>& i) {
        const T& x = trove::get<0>(p);
        const T& y = trove::get<1>(p);
        const T& z = trove::get<2>(p);
        const T r(0.45); /* strictly inside the domain [-0.5,0.5]^3 */
        return x*x + y*y + z*z - r*r;
    }
}
    """

    pmb = ParallelMarchingBlocks(
        3,
        fdef,
        x0=-0.5,
        N=6,
        backend=Backend.CUPY,
    )

    V, I = pmb(to_numpy=True)

    if (V is None) or (I is None):
        msg = "Could not find any vertex, this is a bug."
        logger.error(msg)
        sys.exit(1)

    mesh = trimesh.Trimesh(vertices=V, faces=I)
    mesh.show()
```

### examples/01_animation.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
import os, sys
import open3d
import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(threshold=sys.maxsize, linewidth=sys.maxsize)

if "CUPMB_LOGLEVEL" not in os.environ:
    os.environ["CUPMB_LOGLEVEL"] = "DEBUG"

from cupmb import ParallelMarchingBlocks, Backend, logger

if __name__ == "__main__":

    fdef = """
namespace cupmb {
    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ T scalar(
            const trove::array<T,dim>& p,
            const trove::array<I,dim>& i,
            const T& r) {
        const T& x = trove::get<0>(p);
        const T& y = trove::get<1>(p);
        const T& z = trove::get<2>(p);
        return (x-0.5)*(x-0.5) + (y-0.5)*(y-0.5) + (z-0.5)*(z-0.5) - r*r;
    }
}
    """

    pmb = ParallelMarchingBlocks(
        3,
        fdef,
        backend=Backend.CUPY,
        extra_params=[("r", "T")],
        enable_cuda_std_lib=True,
    )

    V, I = pmb(r=0.5, to_numpy=True)

    if (V is None) or (I is None):
        msg = "Could not find any vertex, this is a bug."
        logger.error(msg)
        sys.exit(1)

    mesh = open3d.geometry.TriangleMesh()
    mesh.vertices = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(V, dtype=np.float64))
    mesh.triangles = open3d.utility.Vector3iVector(np.asarray(I, dtype=np.int32))

    vis = open3d.visualization.Visualizer()
    vis.create_window()

    render_options = vis.get_render_option()
    render_options.mesh_show_back_face = True
    render_options.mesh_show_wireframe = True
    render_options.show_coordinate_frame = True

    vis.add_geometry(mesh)
    vis.add_geometry(pmb.bbox.as_open3d)
    vis.update_renderer()

    frame = 0
    params = {"r": (0.0, 0.75, 1000)}

    running = True
    geometry_removed = False
    while running:
        running = vis.poll_events()

        # kwds contains dynamic parameter values
        kwds = {}
        for pname, (minv, maxv, Nf) in params.items():
            dv = (maxv - minv) / Nf
            N = frame % (2 * Nf)
            if N <= Nf:
                pval = minv + N * dv
            else:
                pval = minv + (2 * Nf - N) * dv
            kwds[pname] = pval

        V, I = pmb(**kwds, to_numpy=True)

        if (V is None) or (I is None):
            if not geometry_removed:
                vis.remove_geometry(mesh, False)
                geometry_removed = True
        else:
            mesh.vertices = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(V, dtype=np.float64))
            mesh.triangles = open3d.utility.Vector3iVector(np.asarray(I, dtype=np.int32))
            if geometry_removed:
                vis.add_geometry(mesh, False)
                geometry_removed = False
            vis.update_geometry(mesh)

        vis.update_renderer()

        frame += 1

    vis.destroy_window()
```

### examples/02_normals.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
import os, sys, time, threading, collections
import open3d
import numpy as np

script_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(__file__))
ext_dir = os.path.join(script_dir, "..", "ext")
sys.path.append(ext_dir)
from vis_gui import AppWindow, gui, rendering

if "CUPMB_LOGLEVEL" not in os.environ:
    os.environ["CUPMB_LOGLEVEL"] = "INFO"

from cupmb import ParallelMarchingBlocks, Backend, VertexProperty, logger

Color = collections.namedtuple("Color", ["red", "green", "blue", "alpha"])


class ExampleAppWindow(AppWindow):

    fdef = r"""
namespace cupmb {
    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ T scalar(
            const trove::array<T,dim>& p,
            const trove::array<I,dim>& i,
            const T& r) {
        const T& x = trove::get<0>(p);
        const T& y = trove::get<1>(p);
        const T& z = trove::get<2>(p);
        return (x-0.5)*(x-0.5) + (y-0.5)*(y-0.5) + (z-0.5)*(z-0.5) - r*r;
    }

    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ trove::array<T,dim> compute_normal(
            const trove::array<T,dim>& p,
            const T& r)
    {
        const T& x = trove::get<0>(p);
        const T& y = trove::get<1>(p);
        const T& z = trove::get<2>(p);
        T nx = x-0.5;
        T ny = y-0.5;
        T nz = z-0.5;
        T nn = rnorm3d(nx, ny, nz);
        return trove::make_array<T>(nx*nn, ny*nn, nz*nn);
    }
}
    """

    def __init__(self, width, height, running):
        super().__init__(width, height)
        self._running = running

        normal = VertexProperty("normal", fname="compute_normal", dtype="T", shape=("dim",))

        self._pmb = ParallelMarchingBlocks(
            3,
            self.fdef,
            N=32,
            backend=Backend.CUPY,
            extra_params=[("r", "T")],
            enable_cuda_std_lib=True,
            vertex_properties=(normal,),
        )

        self._animation_params = {"r": (0.0, 0.75, 1000)}

        # The grid size is requested by the GUI thread and applied by the mesh
        # generation thread (pmb must not be modified while it is being called).
        self._grid_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._grid_size = int(self._pmb.N[0])
        self._requested_grid_size = None

        # Reuse the point size slider of the base window as a grid size slider.
        self._point_size.set_limits(4, 128)
        self._point_size.int_value = self._grid_size
        self._point_size.set_on_value_changed(self._on_grid_change)
        self._point_size_label.text = "Grid size"

        self._load_initial_mesh()

    def _get_animation_parameters(self, frame):
        kwds = {}
        for pname, (minv, maxv, Nf) in self._animation_params.items():
            dv = (maxv - minv) / Nf
            N = (frame % (2 * Nf) + (2 * Nf)) % (2 * Nf)
            if N <= Nf:
                pval = minv + N * dv
            else:
                pval = minv + (2 * Nf - N) * dv
            kwds[pname] = pval
        return kwds

    def _on_grid_change(self, size):
        with self._grid_lock:
            self._grid_size = int(max(4, size))
            self._requested_grid_size = self._grid_size

    def _apply_settings(self):
        super()._apply_settings()
        # the base class resets the slider to the material point size
        # (also called by the base class constructor, before _grid_size exists)
        if hasattr(self, "_grid_size"):
            self._point_size.int_value = self._grid_size

    def _on_menu_open(self):
        logger.info("Cannot open external mesh sorry !")

    def _load_initial_mesh(self):
        V, I, N = self._pmb(**self._get_animation_parameters(-1), to_numpy=True)

        if (V is None) or (I is None):
            msg = "Could not find any vertex, this is a bug."
            logger.error(msg)
            raise RuntimeError(msg)

        bbox = open3d.geometry.AxisAlignedBoundingBox.create_from_points(
            open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray([self._pmb.xmin, self._pmb.xmax], dtype=np.float64)),
        )

        mesh = open3d.geometry.TriangleMesh()

        mesh.vertices = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(V, dtype=np.float64))
        mesh.triangles = open3d.utility.Vector3iVector(np.asarray(I, dtype=np.int32))
        mesh.vertex_normals = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(N, dtype=np.float64))

        bbox_material = rendering.MaterialRecord()

        self._scene.scene.clear_geometry()
        self._scene.scene.add_geometry(
            "mesh",
            mesh,
            self.settings.material,
        )
        self._scene.scene.add_geometry(
            "bbox",
            bbox,
            bbox_material,
        )

        self._scene.setup_camera(60, bbox, bbox.get_center())
        self._on_material_color(Color(0.0, 0.6, 0.2, 1.0))
        self._on_use_sun(False)

        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._generate_mesh)
        self._thread.start()

    def _generate_mesh(self):
        frame = 0
        self._can_update = True
        while self._running[0]:
            with self._grid_lock:
                grid_size, self._requested_grid_size = (
                    self._requested_grid_size,
                    None,
                )
            if grid_size is not None:
                self._pmb.N = grid_size

            V, I, N = self._pmb(**self._get_animation_parameters(frame), to_numpy=True)

            if (V is None) or (I is None):
                mesh = None
            else:
                mesh = open3d.geometry.TriangleMesh()
                mesh.vertices = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(V, dtype=np.float64))
                mesh.triangles = open3d.utility.Vector3iVector(np.asarray(I, dtype=np.int32))
                mesh.vertex_normals = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(N, dtype=np.float64))
            self._mesh = mesh

            while self._running[0] and not self._can_update:
                time.sleep(0.001)
            self._can_update = False

            gui.Application.instance.post_to_main_thread(self.window, self.update_geometries)

            frame += 1

    def update_geometries(self):
        try:
            if self._scene.scene.has_geometry("mesh"):
                self._scene.scene.remove_geometry("mesh")
            if self._mesh is not None:
                self._scene.scene.add_geometry(
                    "mesh",
                    self._mesh,
                    self.settings.material,
                )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception(e)

        self._can_update = True


def main():
    gui.Application.instance.initialize()

    # dirty trick to know when to exit thread...
    running = [True]

    w = ExampleAppWindow(1024, 768, running)
    if len(sys.argv) > 1:
        path = sys.argv[1]
        if os.path.exists(path):
            w.load(path)
        else:
            w.window.show_message_box("Error", "Could not open file '" + path + "'")

    # Run the event loop. This will not return until the last window is closed.
    gui.Application.instance.run()

    running[0] = False


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### examples/03_glinterop_cupy.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
import os, sys, ctypes

try:
    from cuda.bindings import runtime as cudart  # cuda-python >= 12.6
except ImportError:
    from cuda import cudart  # older cuda-python

if "CUPMB_LOGLEVEL" not in os.environ:
    os.environ["CUPMB_LOGLEVEL"] = "INFO"

from cupmb import ParallelMarchingBlocks, BufferType, Backend

import numpy as np

import pyrr
import glfw
from OpenGL.GL import *  # noqa F403
import OpenGL.GL.shaders

VERTEX_SHADER = """
#version 330

in vec3 position;

uniform mat4 transform;

void main() {
    gl_Position = transform * vec4(position, 1.0f);
}
"""


FRAGMENT_SHADER = """
#version 330

out vec4 outColor;

void main() {
    outColor = vec4(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
}
"""

CUPMB_SCALAR = """
namespace cupmb {
    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ T scalar(
            const trove::array<T,dim>& p,
            const trove::array<I,dim>& i,
            const T& r) {
        const T& x = trove::get<0>(p);
        const T& y = trove::get<1>(p);
        const T& z = trove::get<2>(p);
        return x*x + y*y + z*z - r*r;
    }
}
"""


def setup_buffers():
    max_vertices = 10 * 1024 * 1024
    max_triangles = 10 * 1024 * 1024

    ftype = np.float32
    itype = np.uint32

    vertex_bytes = 3 * max_vertices * ftype().nbytes
    index_bytes = 3 * max_triangles * itype().nbytes

    pmb = ParallelMarchingBlocks(
        3,
        CUPMB_SCALAR,
        ftype=ftype,
        itype=itype,
        x0=-0.5,
        N=32,
        extra_params=[("r", "T")],
        backend=Backend.CUPY,
    )

    flags = cudart.cudaGraphicsRegisterFlags.cudaGraphicsRegisterFlagsWriteDiscard

    VBO = glGenBuffers(1)
    glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, VBO)
    glBufferData(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, vertex_bytes, None, GL_DYNAMIC_DRAW)
    shared_vertex_buffer = pmb.backend.CudaOpenGLMappedBuffer(VBO, flags)

    EBO = glGenBuffers(1)
    glBindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, EBO)
    glBufferData(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, index_bytes, None, GL_DYNAMIC_DRAW)
    shared_index_buffer = pmb.backend.CudaOpenGLMappedBuffer(EBO, flags)

    pmb.vertex_buffer = shared_vertex_buffer.map()
    pmb.simplex_buffer = shared_index_buffer.map()
    pmb.lock_buffer(BufferType.VERTICES)
    pmb.lock_buffer(BufferType.SIMPLICES)

    assert pmb.allocated_vertex_count == max_vertices, (
        pmb.allocated_vertex_count,
        max_vertices,
    )
    assert pmb.allocated_simplices_count == max_triangles, (
        pmb.allocated_simplices_count,
        max_triangles,
    )

    pmb.setup(0.5)

    shared_vertex_buffer.unmap()
    shared_index_buffer.unmap()

    def update_buffers(
        t,
        shared_index_buffer=shared_index_buffer,
        shared_vertex_buffer=shared_vertex_buffer,
        pmb=pmb,
    ):
        r = (t / 10.0) % 1.0
        r = (1 - r) if r > 0.5 else r

        pmb.replace_buffer(BufferType.VERTICES, shared_vertex_buffer.map())
        pmb.replace_buffer(BufferType.SIMPLICES, shared_index_buffer.map())

        V, I = pmb(r=r)

        shared_vertex_buffer.unmap()
        shared_index_buffer.unmap()

        Ntriangles = 0 if (I is None) else I.shape[0]
        assert Ntriangles <= max_triangles, (Ntriangles, max_triangles)

        return Ntriangles

    def unregister_buffers(
        shared_index_buffer=shared_index_buffer,
        shared_vertex_buffer=shared_vertex_buffer,
    ):
        shared_vertex_buffer.unregister()
        shared_index_buffer.unregister()

    return VBO, EBO, update_buffers, unregister_buffers


def main():
    if not glfw.init():
        return
    title = "Cupmb Cuda/OpenGL interop example"
    window = glfw.create_window(800, 800, title, None, None)

    if not window:
        glfw.terminate()
        return

    glfw.make_context_current(window)
    glfw.swap_interval(0)

    shader = OpenGL.GL.shaders.compileProgram(
        OpenGL.GL.shaders.compileShader(VERTEX_SHADER, GL_VERTEX_SHADER),
        OpenGL.GL.shaders.compileShader(FRAGMENT_SHADER, GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER),
    )

    VBO, EBO, update_buffers, unregister_buffers = setup_buffers()

    position = glGetAttribLocation(shader, "position")

    glUseProgram(shader)
    glEnable(GL_DEPTH_TEST)

    fps = 0
    nframes = 0
    last_time = glfw.get_time()
    while not glfw.window_should_close(window):
        t = glfw.get_time()
        Ntriangles = update_buffers(t)

        dt = t - last_time
        if dt >= 1.0:
            fps = nframes / dt
            last_time = t
            nframes = 0

        width, height = glfw.get_window_size(window)
        glViewport(0, 0, width, height)

        glClearColor(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
        glClear(GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT | GL_DEPTH_BUFFER_BIT)
        glPolygonMode(GL_FRONT_AND_BACK, GL_LINE)

        rot_x = pyrr.Matrix44.from_x_rotation(0)
        rot_y = pyrr.Matrix44.from_y_rotation(t)

        transformLoc = glGetUniformLocation(shader, "transform")
        glUniformMatrix4fv(transformLoc, 1, GL_FALSE, rot_x * rot_y)

        glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, VBO)
        glBindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, EBO)
        glEnableVertexAttribArray(position)
        glVertexAttribPointer(position, 3, GL_FLOAT, GL_FALSE, 12, ctypes.c_void_p(0))
        glDrawElements(GL_TRIANGLES, int(3 * Ntriangles), GL_UNSIGNED_INT, None)

        glfw.swap_buffers(window)
        glfw.poll_events()
        glfw.set_window_title(window, f"{title} ({fps:.1f} fps)")
        nframes += 1

    unregister_buffers()

    glfw.terminate()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

    os.environ["CUPMB_LOGLEVEL"] = "INFO"

import numpy as np

import glfw

import OpenGL.GL.shaders

#version 330

in vec3 position;

uniform mat4 transform;

void main() {
    gl_Position = transform * vec4(position, 1.0f);

"""

#version 330

out vec4 outColor;

void main() {
    outColor = vec4(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);

"""

namespace cupmb {
    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ T scalar(
            const trove::array<T,dim>& p,
            const trove::array<I,dim>& i,

def setup_buffers():
    import pycuda

    max_vertices = 10 * 1024 * 1024
    max_triangles = 10 * 1024 * 1024

    ftype = np.float32

    pmb.lock_buffer(BufferType.SIMPLICES)

    shared_index_buffer.unmap()

    def unregister_buffers(
        shared_index_buffer=shared_index_buffer,
        shared_vertex_buffer=shared_vertex_buffer,

### examples/04_isolines.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
import os, sys
import numpy as np

import matplotlib
import matplotlib.collections
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

np.set_printoptions(threshold=sys.maxsize, linewidth=sys.maxsize)

if "CUPMB_LOGLEVEL" not in os.environ:
    os.environ["CUPMB_LOGLEVEL"] = "DEBUG"

from cupmb import ParallelMarchingBlocks, Backend, logger


def as_line_collection(V, I):
    lines = [] if (V is None or I is None) else [[tuple(V[j]) for j in i] for i in I]
    return matplotlib.collections.LineCollection(lines, lw=2, color="r", zorder=3)


def animate_plot(pmb, fig, ax, level):
    V, I, S = pmb(t=0, level=level, to_numpy=True)
    if V is None:
        msg = "Could not find any vertex, this is a bug."
        logger.error(msg)
        sys.exit(1)
    lc = as_line_collection(V, I)

    x0 = pmb.x0
    N = pmb.N
    L = pmb.L
    dx = pmb.dx

    x = x0[0] + dx[0] * np.arange(N[0] + 1)
    y = x0[1] + dx[1] * np.arange(N[1] + 1)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(x, y)

    ax.set_title(f"Marching squares with Nx={N[0]} and Ny={N[1]} (black = matplotlib.contour, red = cupmb)")
    img = ax.imshow(
        S,
        vmin=S.min(),
        vmax=S.max(),
        extent=[
            x0[0] - dx[0] / 2,
            x0[0] + dx[0] / 2 + L[0],
            x0[1] - dx[1] / 2,
            x0[1] + dx[1] / 2 + L[1],
        ],
        interpolation="none",
        origin="lower",
        zorder=1,
    )
    contours = ax.contour(X, Y, S, levels=[0], colors=["k"], linewidths=[5], zorder=2)
    ax.add_collection(lc)
    ax.set_xlim(x0[0], x0[0] + L[0])
    ax.set_ylim(x0[1], x0[1] + L[1])
    fig.show()

    it = 1
    dt = 0.01
    while plt.fignum_exists(fig.number):
        V, I, S = pmb(t=it * dt, level=level, to_numpy=True)

        img.set_data(S)

        lc.remove()
        lc = as_line_collection(V, I)
        ax.add_collection(lc)

        contours.remove()
        contours = ax.contour(X, Y, S, levels=[0], colors=["k"], linewidths=[5], zorder=2)

        fig.canvas.draw()
        fig.canvas.flush_events()
        it += 1


if __name__ == "__main__":

    fdef = r"""
namespace cupmb {
    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ T scalar(
            const trove::array<T,dim>& p,
            const trove::array<I,dim>& i,
            const T& level, const T& t) {
        const T& x = trove::get<0>(p);
        const T& y = trove::get<1>(p);
        return sin(x*x - y*y + t) * cos(2*x+x*y/4 - t) - level;
    }
}
    """

    level = 0.2

    pmb = ParallelMarchingBlocks(
        2,
        fdef,
        L=3,
        N=128,
        backend=Backend.CUPY,
        extra_params=[("level", "T"), ("t", "T")],
        export_scalar_field=True,
        enable_cuda_std_lib=True,
    )

    fig, ax = plt.subplots(nrows=1, ncols=1, figsize=(12, 12))
    animate_plot(pmb, fig, ax, level)
```

### examples/05_moving_sphere.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
import os, sys, collections
import open3d
import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(threshold=sys.maxsize, linewidth=sys.maxsize)

if "CUPMB_LOGLEVEL" not in os.environ:
    os.environ["CUPMB_LOGLEVEL"] = "DEBUG"

from cupmb import ParallelMarchingBlocks, ExtraParameter, Backend, logger


class PingPong:
    def __init__(self, pmin, pmax, freq):
        assert freq > 0, freq
        self.pmin, self.pmax, self.freq, self.frame = (pmin, pmax, freq, -1)

    def __call__(self):
        self.frame += 1
        dp = (self.pmax - self.pmin) / self.freq
        N = self.frame % (2 * self.freq)
        if N <= self.freq:
            val = self.pmin + N * dp
        else:
            val = self.pmin + (2 * self.freq - N) * dp
        return val


if __name__ == "__main__":

    fdef = """
namespace cupmb {
    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ T scalar(
            const trove::array<T,dim>& p,
            const trove::array<I,dim>& i,
            const T& x0, const T& y0, const T& z0) {
        const T& x = trove::get<0>(p);
        const T& y = trove::get<1>(p);
        const T& z = trove::get<2>(p);
        return (x-x0)*(x-x0) + (y-y0)*(y-y0) + (z-z0)*(z-z0) - 0.25*0.25;
    }
}
    """

    extra_params = collections.OrderedDict()
    extra_params["x0"] = ExtraParameter("T", PingPong(0.25, 0.75, 113))
    extra_params["y0"] = ExtraParameter("T", PingPong(0.25, 0.75, 157))
    extra_params["z0"] = ExtraParameter("T", PingPong(0.25, 0.75, 193))

    pmb = ParallelMarchingBlocks(
        3,
        fdef,
        backend=Backend.CUPY,
        extra_params=extra_params,
        enable_cuda_std_lib=True,
    )
    bbox = pmb.bbox.as_open3d

    # Callables are not called when parameters are given
    # This is usefull for buffer initialization
    V, I = pmb(x0=0.5, y0=0.5, z0=0.5, to_numpy=True)

    if (V is None) or (I is None):
        msg = "Could not find any vertex, this is a bug."
        logger.error(msg)
        sys.exit(1)

    mesh = open3d.geometry.TriangleMesh()
    mesh.vertices = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(V, dtype=np.float64))
    mesh.triangles = open3d.utility.Vector3iVector(np.asarray(I, dtype=np.int32))

    vis = open3d.visualization.Visualizer()
    vis.create_window()

    render_options = vis.get_render_option()
    render_options.mesh_show_back_face = True
    render_options.mesh_show_wireframe = True
    render_options.show_coordinate_frame = True

    vis.add_geometry(mesh)
    vis.add_geometry(bbox)
    vis.update_renderer()

    running = True
    geometry_removed = False
    while running:
        running = vis.poll_events()

        # Call the callables for all missing extra parameters
        V, I = pmb(to_numpy=True)

        if (V is None) or (I is None):
            if not geometry_removed:
                vis.remove_geometry(mesh, False)
                geometry_removed = True
        else:
            mesh.vertices = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(V, dtype=np.float64))
            mesh.triangles = open3d.utility.Vector3iVector(np.asarray(I, dtype=np.int32))
            if geometry_removed:
                vis.add_geometry(mesh, False)
                geometry_removed = False
            vis.update_geometry(mesh)

        vis.update_renderer()

    vis.destroy_window()
```

### examples/06_moving_bbox.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
import os, sys, collections
import open3d
import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(threshold=sys.maxsize, linewidth=sys.maxsize)

if "CUPMB_LOGLEVEL" not in os.environ:
    os.environ["CUPMB_LOGLEVEL"] = "DEBUG"

from cupmb import (
    ParallelMarchingBlocks,
    AllocationPolicy,
    ExtraParameter,
    Backend,
    logger,
)


class PingPong:
    def __init__(self, pmin, pmax, freq):
        assert freq > 0, freq
        self.pmin, self.pmax, self.freq, self.frame = (pmin, pmax, freq, -1)

    def __call__(self):
        self.frame += 1
        dp = (self.pmax - self.pmin) / self.freq
        N = self.frame % (2 * self.freq)
        if N <= self.freq:
            val = self.pmin + N * dp
        else:
            val = self.pmin + (2 * self.freq - N) * dp
        return val


if __name__ == "__main__":

    fdef = """
namespace cupmb {
    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ T scalar(
            const trove::array<T,dim>& p,
            const trove::array<I,dim>& i,
            const T& x0, const T& y0, const T& z0) {
        const T& x = trove::get<0>(p);
        const T& y = trove::get<1>(p);
        const T& z = trove::get<2>(p);
        return (x-x0)*(x-x0) + (y-y0)*(y-y0) + (z-z0)*(z-z0) - 0.25*0.25;
    }
}
    """

    extra_params = collections.OrderedDict()
    extra_params["x0"] = ExtraParameter("T", PingPong(0.25, 0.75, 113))
    extra_params["y0"] = ExtraParameter("T", PingPong(0.25, 0.75, 157))
    extra_params["z0"] = ExtraParameter("T", PingPong(0.25, 0.75, 193))

    pmb = ParallelMarchingBlocks(
        3,
        fdef,
        N=32,
        backend=Backend.CUPY,
        extra_params=extra_params,
        enable_cuda_std_lib=True,
        default_allocation_policy=AllocationPolicy.ALLOCATE_EXACT_SIZE,
    )

    scene_bbox = pmb.bbox.as_open3d
    moving_bbox = pmb.bbox.as_open3d
    moving_bbox.color = (1.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    def update_bbox(bbox, x0, y0, z0, moving_bbox=moving_bbox):
        center = np.asarray([x0, y0, z0], dtype=bbox.dtype)
        bbox.xmin = center - 0.25
        bbox.xmax = center + 0.25

        moving_bbox.min_bound = np.asarray(bbox.xmin, dtype=np.float64)
        moving_bbox.max_bound = np.asarray(bbox.xmax, dtype=np.float64)

    pmb.update_bbox = update_bbox

    # Callables are not called when parameters are explicitely given
    # This can be used for buffer initialization during setup (or first call)
    V, I = pmb(x0=0.5, y0=0.5, z0=0.5, to_numpy=True)

    # All buffers can be locked to check that they are never reallocated
    pmb.lock_all_buffers()

    if (V is None) or (I is None):
        msg = "Could not find any vertex, this is a bug."
        logger.error(msg)
        sys.exit(1)

    mesh = open3d.geometry.TriangleMesh()
    mesh.vertices = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(V, dtype=np.float64))
    mesh.triangles = open3d.utility.Vector3iVector(np.asarray(I, dtype=np.int32))

    vis = open3d.visualization.Visualizer()
    vis.create_window()

    render_options = vis.get_render_option()
    render_options.mesh_show_back_face = True
    render_options.mesh_show_wireframe = True
    render_options.show_coordinate_frame = True

    vis.add_geometry(mesh)
    vis.add_geometry(scene_bbox)
    vis.add_geometry(moving_bbox)
    vis.update_renderer()

    running = True
    geometry_removed = False
    while running:
        running = vis.poll_events()

        # Call the callables for all missing extra parameters
        V, I = pmb(to_numpy=True)

        if (V is None) or (I is None):
            if not geometry_removed:
                vis.remove_geometry(mesh, False)
                geometry_removed = True
        else:
            mesh.vertices = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(V, dtype=np.float64))
            mesh.triangles = open3d.utility.Vector3iVector(np.asarray(I, dtype=np.int32))
            if geometry_removed:
                vis.add_geometry(mesh, False)
                geometry_removed = False
            vis.update_geometry(mesh)

        vis.update_geometry(moving_bbox)
        vis.update_renderer()

    vis.destroy_window()
```

### examples/07_predicate.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#

# Block predicate example: a user defined predicate discards the blocks of the domain
# that certainly do not contain any part of the isosurface, before any scalar is evaluated.
#
# The predicate is evaluated with the affine arithmetic library cusvna (https://github.com/keckj/cusvna):
# the shape function below is written once as a template and evaluated on floating point numbers
# (scalar field), on affine forms cusvna::AF1<T,3> (predicate) and on first order autodiff numbers
# (https://github.com/keckj/autodiff, git submodule ext/autodiff) for the normals. Functions such
# as sqr or sin are found by argument dependent lookup.
#
# The resulting mesh is finally displayed with open3d at a lower resolution (--display-N, --no-display),
# with smooth shading from exact normals computed on the GPU as a vertex property.
#
import os
import sys
import argparse

if "CUPMB_LOGLEVEL" not in os.environ:
    os.environ["CUPMB_LOGLEVEL"] = "INFO"

import numpy as np

from cupmb import (
    ParallelMarchingBlocks,
    Backend,
    AllocationPolicy,
    AutotuningRigor,
    VertexProperty,
    logger,
)
from cupmb.utils.units import time2str

AUTODIFF_INCLUDE_DIR = os.path.realpath(
    os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "ext", "autodiff")
)

# Scalar field definition (fdef): a templated shape function S shape<T>(S x, S y, S z)
# where S is either T (float/double), an affine form or an autodiff number.
fdef = r"""
#include <autodiff/forward/real.hpp>

namespace cupmb {
    /* scalar and autodiff version of sqr, the affine form version cusvna::sqr is found by ADL
     * (and preferred by partial ordering since it is more specialized) */
    template <typename S>
    __device__ __forceinline__ S sqr(const S& x) { return x*x; }

    /* Three interlaced tori, stretched along y and z and perturbed along x so that the
     * shape is not invariant under any axis permutation or reflection. */
    template <typename T, typename S>
    __device__ __forceinline__ S shape(const S& x, const S& y, const S& z) {
        const T c = T(0.85) * T(0.85);
        const T o = T(1);

        const S xx = sqr(x);
        const S yy = sqr(T(1.05) * y);
        const S zz = sqr(T(1.10) * z);

        return (sqr(xx + yy - c) + sqr(zz - o))
             * (sqr(yy + zz - c) + sqr(xx - o))
             * (sqr(zz + xx - c) + sqr(yy - o)) - T(0.01)
             + T(0.05) * sin(T(3) * x);
    }

    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ T scalar(
            const trove::array<T,dim>& p,
            const trove::array<I,dim>& i) {
        return shape<T>(trove::get<0>(p), trove::get<1>(p), trove::get<2>(p));
    }

    /* Normal vertex property (display only): normalized exact gradient by forward automatic
     * differentiation (one evaluation per direction), pointing from negative to positive values (outwards). */
    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ trove::array<T,dim> compute_normal(const trove::array<T,dim>& p) {
        using autodiff::at;
        using autodiff::derivative;
        using autodiff::wrt;
        using real = autodiff::Real<1,T>;
        const auto f = [](const real& x, const real& y, const real& z) { return shape<T>(x, y, z); };
        real x = trove::get<0>(p);
        real y = trove::get<1>(p);
        real z = trove::get<2>(p);
        const T nx = derivative(f, wrt(x), at(x, y, z));
        const T ny = derivative(f, wrt(y), at(x, y, z));
        const T nz = derivative(f, wrt(z), at(x, y, z));
        const T nn = rnorm3d(nx, ny, nz);
        return trove::make_array<T>(nx*nn, ny*nn, nz*nn);
    }
}
"""

# Predicate definition (pdef): returns false only if the block domain certainly does not
# contain any zero of the shape function. domain.a[k] and domain.b[k] are the lower and upper
# bounds of the block along axis k (0=x, 1=y, 2=z).
pdef = r"""
#include <cusvna/af1.cuh>

namespace cupmb {
    template <typename T, typename I, I dim>
    __device__ __forceinline__ bool may_contain_zeros(const BoundingBox<T,dim>& domain) {
        using Interval = cusvna::Interval<T>;
        using AF = cusvna::AF1<T,3>;  /* 3 input noise symbols: x, y and z */

        const AF x = AF::template from_interval<0>(Interval(domain.a[0], domain.b[0]));
        const AF y = AF::template from_interval<1>(Interval(domain.a[1], domain.b[1]));
        const AF z = AF::template from_interval<2>(Interval(domain.a[2], domain.b[2]));

        return possibly_zero(shape<T>(x, y, z));
    }
}
"""


def benchmark(pmb, runs):
    pmb()  # preallocate buffers (and autotune)
    vertices, triangles, *_ = pmb()
    ntris = int(triangles.shape[0])
    times = [pmb(return_elapsed_time=True)[-1] for _ in range(runs)]
    return float(np.median(times)), ntris


def display(N):
    """Mesh the shape at resolution N with the predicate and display it with open3d."""
    import open3d

    normal = VertexProperty("normal", fname="compute_normal", dtype="T", shape=("dim",))
    pmb = ParallelMarchingBlocks(
        3,
        fdef,
        pdef=pdef,
        x0=-1.2,
        L=2.4,
        N=N,
        ftype=np.float32,
        autotuning_rigor=AutotuningRigor.ESTIMATE,
        enable_cuda_std_lib=True,
        extra_build_options=(f"-I{AUTODIFF_INCLUDE_DIR}",),
        vertex_properties=(normal,),
    )
    V, I, normals = pmb(to_numpy=True)
    logger.info(f"Displaying the mesh obtained with N={N} ({V.shape[0]} vertices, {I.shape[0]} triangles).")

    mesh = open3d.geometry.TriangleMesh()
    mesh.vertices = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(V, dtype=np.float64))
    mesh.triangles = open3d.utility.Vector3iVector(np.asarray(I, dtype=np.int32))
    mesh.vertex_normals = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(np.asarray(normals, dtype=np.float64))
    mesh.compute_triangle_normals()  # the legacy open3d renderer disables lighting without triangle normals
    # color by height along z, the axis that is stretched the most
    z = np.asarray(V[:, 2], dtype=np.float64)
    z = (z - z.min()) / max(np.ptp(z), 1e-12)
    colors = np.stack([0.25 + 0.6 * z, 0.45 * np.ones_like(z), 0.85 - 0.6 * z], axis=-1)
    mesh.vertex_colors = open3d.utility.Vector3dVector(colors)

    open3d.visualization.draw_geometries(
        [mesh, pmb.bbox.as_open3d], window_name=f"cupmb predicate example (N={N})", width=1280, height=960
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Block predicate benchmark (cusvna affine arithmetic).")
    parser.add_argument("-N", type=int, default=2048, help="grid size, N=2048 requires ~12GB of VRAM")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--rigor",
        default="MEASURE",
        choices=[r.name for r in AutotuningRigor],
        help="autotuning rigor (ESTIMATE: ~8s, MEASURE: ~45s, EXHAUSTIVE: ~85s on a RTX 5090)",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--runs", type=int, default=10, help="number of timed runs")
    parser.add_argument("--display-N", type=int, default=512, help="grid size of the displayed mesh")
    parser.add_argument("--no-display", action="store_true", help="do not display the mesh with open3d")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if not os.path.isdir(os.path.join(AUTODIFF_INCLUDE_DIR, "autodiff")):
        logger.error("Cannot find the autodiff submodule, run git submodule update --init --recursive.")
        sys.exit(1)

    N = args.N
    logger.info(f"Benchmarking naive block mask extraction vs predicate based block extraction for N={N}.")

    pmb = ParallelMarchingBlocks(
        3,
        fdef,
        x0=-1.2,
        L=2.4,
        N=N,
        ftype=np.float32,
        backend=Backend.CUPY,
        default_allocation_policy=AllocationPolicy.ALLOCATE_EXACT_SIZE,
        autotuning_rigor=AutotuningRigor[args.rigor],
        enable_cuda_std_lib=True,
        # Disable FMA contraction so that the scalar field is evaluated identically by
        # all kernel variants: otherwise the sign of the few scalars that are very close
        # to zero may depend on the kernel, and so does the triangle count.
        extra_build_options=("--fmad=false", f"-I{AUTODIFF_INCLUDE_DIR}"),
    )

    t0, n0 = benchmark(pmb, args.runs)

    pmb.pdef = pdef
    t1, n1 = benchmark(pmb, args.runs)

    logger.info(
        f"Kernel launcher finished in {time2str(t0)} without predicate ({n0} triangles, median of {args.runs} runs)."
    )
    logger.info(
        f"Kernel launcher finished in {time2str(t1)} with predicate ({n1} triangles, median of {args.runs} runs)."
    )
    logger.info(f"Speedup factor is {t0/t1:2.1f}.")

    if n0 != n1:
        logger.error(f"Triangle count mismatch: {n0} without predicate vs {n1} with predicate.")
        raise SystemExit(1)

    if not args.no_display:
        del pmb  # release the device memory of the benchmark
        display(args.display_N)
```

### examples/08_levelset.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
