#include <trove/array.h>


#ifdef CUPMB_ENABLE_CUDA_STD_LIB
#if defined(__CUDACC_RTC__)
/* NVRTC build */
#include <cuda/std/tuple>
namespace cuda {
    namespace std {
        template <typename T, int K>
        struct tuple_size<trove::array<T,K>> : integral_constant<size_t, K> {};

        template <size_t I, typename T, int K>
        struct tuple_element<I, trove::array<T,K>> { using type = T; };
    } /* end of namespace cuda::std */
} /* end of namespace cuda */
namespace std {
    using cuda::std::tuple;
    /*using cuda::std::tuple_size;*/
    /*using cuda::std::tuple_element;*/
} /* end of namespace std */
#else
/* NVCC build */
#include <tuple>
namespace std {
    template <typename T, int K>
    struct tuple_size<trove::array<T,K>> : integral_constant<size_t, K> {};

    template <size_t I, typename T, int K>
    struct tuple_element<I, trove::array<T,K>> { using type = T; };
} /* end of namespace std */
#endif /* defined(__CUDACC_RTC__) */
#endif /* CUPMB_ENABLE_CUDA_STD_LIB */


namespace cupmb {

__forceinline__ __device__ uint2 operator-(const uint2 &a, const uint2 &b) {
    return uint2{a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y};
}
__forceinline__ __device__ ulonglong2 operator-(const ulonglong2 &a, const ulonglong2 &b) {
    return ulonglong2{a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y};
}


__forceinline__ __device__ uint2& operator+=(uint2 &a, const uint2 &b) {
    a.x += b.x;
    a.y += b.y;
    return a;
}
__forceinline__ __device__ ulonglong2& operator+=(ulonglong2 &a, const ulonglong2 &b) {
    a.x += b.x;
    a.y += b.y;
    return a;
}

using ::atomicAdd;

__forceinline__ __device__ uint2 atomicAdd(uint2* address, uint2 val) {
    uint2 out;
    out.x = atomicAdd(reinterpret_cast<unsigned int*>(address)+0, val.x);
    out.y = atomicAdd(reinterpret_cast<unsigned int*>(address)+1, val.y);
    return out;
}
__forceinline__ __device__ ulonglong2 atomicAdd(ulonglong2* address, ulonglong2 val) {
    ulonglong2 out;
    out.x = atomicAdd(reinterpret_cast<unsigned long long int*>(address)+0, val.x);
    out.y = atomicAdd(reinterpret_cast<unsigned long long int*>(address)+1, val.y);
    return out;
}

template <typename I> struct I2 { using type = void; };
template <> struct I2<unsigned int> { using type = uint2; };
template <> struct I2<unsigned long long int> { using type = ulonglong2; };

template <class T, T v>
struct integral_constant {
  static constexpr T value = v;
  typedef T value_type;
  typedef integral_constant<T,v> type;
  constexpr __device__ operator T() { return v; }
};

typedef integral_constant<bool, false> false_type;
typedef integral_constant<bool, true> true_type;

template<class T, class U>
struct is_same : false_type {};

template<class T>
struct is_same<T, T> : true_type {};

template <typename T>
__forceinline__ __device__ T shfl_up_sync(unsigned int mask, T var, unsigned int delta) {
    if constexpr(is_same<T,ulonglong2>::value || is_same<T,uint2>::value) {
        var.x = __shfl_up_sync(mask, var.x, delta);
        var.y = __shfl_up_sync(mask, var.y, delta);
        return var;
    }
    else {
        return __shfl_up_sync(mask, var, delta);
    }
    __builtin_unreachable();
}


/* Type-correct quiet NaN and positive infinity (avoids double promotion of nan("") in float kernels). */
template <typename T> __forceinline__ __device__ T quiet_nan();
template <> __forceinline__ __device__ float  quiet_nan<float>()  { return __int_as_float(0x7fffffff); }
template <> __forceinline__ __device__ double quiet_nan<double>() { return __longlong_as_double(0x7ff8000000000000ll); }

template <typename T> __forceinline__ __device__ T positive_infinity();
template <> __forceinline__ __device__ float  positive_infinity<float>()  { return __int_as_float(0x7f800000); }
template <> __forceinline__ __device__ double positive_infinity<double>() { return __longlong_as_double(0x7ff0000000000000ll); }


/* NaN policy (shared by all dimensions):
 *  - NaN scalar values are considered positive, i.e. outside (s<=0 is false for NaN).
 *  - For block masks, NaN values are replaced by +infinity so that a block containing both
 *    negative and NaN values is considered active (NaN is reserved for out of domain points,
 *    which are ignored by the min/max reductions).
 *  - A vertex is never interpolated with a non-finite value: if the interpolation parameter
 *    s0/(s0-s1) is not finite, the vertex is snapped to the endpoint with a finite scalar value
 *    (to the second endpoint if the first one is not finite). */
template <typename T>
__forceinline__ __device__ T mask_value(const T v) {
    return (isnan(v) ? positive_infinity<T>() : v);
}

template <typename T>
__forceinline__ __device__ T edge_interpolation_parameter(const T s0, const T s1) {
    const T t = s0/(s0-s1);
    return (isfinite(t) ? t : (isfinite(s0) ? T(0) : T(1)));
}


/* Returns the number of bits required to represent x (0 for x=0). */
template<typename I>
__forceinline__ __device__ I number_of_bits(const I x) {
    if constexpr (sizeof(I) == 8) {
        return I(64 - __clzll((long long)(x)));
    }
    else {
        return I(32 - __clz(int(x)));
    }
}

/* Returns ceil(log2(v)), with log_of_next_pow2(0) = log_of_next_pow2(1) = 0. */
template<typename I>
__forceinline__ __device__ I log_of_next_pow2(const I v) {
    return v <= 1 ? I(0) : number_of_bits(I(v - 1));
}

/* Number of logical blocks along an axis with N cells and B threads per block (blocks overlap by one point). */
template<typename I>
__forceinline__ __device__ I number_of_blocks(const I N, const I B) {
    return (N + B - 2)/(B - 1);
}


template <typename I, unsigned int warps_per_block, unsigned int warpsize>
__device__ I prefix_sum(I prefix, const unsigned int laneid, const unsigned int warpid,
        I* const __restrict volatile warp_offset, I* const __restrict volatile global_block_offset) {
    const I Z = {};

    /* intra warp prefix scan, all warp shuffle scan their data */
    I local_prefix(prefix);
    {
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned int offset=1u; offset<warpsize; offset<<=1u) {
            const I other_prefix = shfl_up_sync<I>(0xffffffff, local_prefix, offset);
            if (laneid >= offset) {
                local_prefix += other_prefix;
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
        if ((laneid == warpsize-1u) && (warpid<warps_per_block)) {
            warp_offset[warpid] = local_prefix;
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }

    /* intra block prefix scan into shared memory, only first warp participates */
    {
        if (warpid == 0) {
            I warp_prefix = (laneid >= warps_per_block ? Z : warp_offset[laneid]);
            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned int offset=1; offset<warpsize; offset<<=1u) {
                const I other_prefix = shfl_up_sync<I>(0xffffffff, warp_prefix, offset);
                if (laneid >= offset) {
                    warp_prefix += other_prefix;
                }
            }
            if (laneid < warps_per_block) {
                warp_offset[laneid] = warp_prefix;
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
        local_prefix += (warpid==0 ? Z : warp_offset[warpid-1]);
    }

    /* determine an inter block offset by using atomic operation in global memory (order does not matter) */
    {
        if ((warpid == 0) && (laneid == 0)) {
            warp_offset[warps_per_block] = atomicAdd(global_block_offset, warp_offset[warps_per_block-1]);
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }

    /* inter block prefix scan */
    local_prefix += warp_offset[warps_per_block];
    __syncthreads();

    return local_prefix - prefix;
}

template <typename T, typename I, unsigned int warps_per_block, unsigned int warpsize>
__device__ void compute_block_mask(const T local_value, const I bid,
        const unsigned int laneid, const unsigned int warpid,
        T *const __restrict minbuf, T *const __restrict maxbuf,
        unsigned char *const __restrict block_mask) {

    T minval(local_value);
    T maxval(local_value);

    /* intra warp reduction */
    {
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned int offset=warpsize/2u; offset>0u; offset>>=1u) {
            minval = min(minval, __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, minval, offset));
            maxval = max(maxval, __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, maxval, offset));
        }
        if (laneid == 0) {
            minbuf[warpid] = minval;
            maxbuf[warpid] = maxval;
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }

    /* intra block reduction */
    if (warpid == 0) {
        minval = (laneid >= warps_per_block ? quiet_nan<T>() : minbuf[laneid]);
        maxval = (laneid >= warps_per_block ? quiet_nan<T>() : maxbuf[laneid]);

        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned int offset=warpsize/2u; offset>0u; offset>>=1u) {
            minval = min(minval, __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, minval, offset));
            maxval = max(maxval, __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, maxval, offset));
        }
        if (laneid == 0) {
            const bool block_is_active = (minval <= T(0)) && (maxval >= T(0)) && (minval < maxval);
            block_mask[bid] = (unsigned char)(block_is_active);
        }
    }
}


template<typename I, I Nthreads, unsigned int warpsize>
__launch_bounds__(Nthreads)
__global__ void countActiveBlocks(
        const I Nblocks,
        const unsigned char *const __restrict block_mask,
                          I *const __restrict active_blocks_count) {
    static_assert(Nthreads%warpsize==0, "Nthreads%warpsize != 0");
    static_assert(Nthreads/warpsize<=warpsize, "Nthreads/warpsize > warpsize");
    constexpr unsigned int warps_per_block = Nthreads/warpsize;
    __shared__ I block_count[warps_per_block];

    const unsigned int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned int bid = blockIdx.x;
    const unsigned int laneid = tid % warpsize;
    const unsigned int warpid = tid / warpsize;

    const I block_index = bid*Nthreads + tid;
    const bool block_is_active = (block_index >= Nblocks ? false : block_mask[block_index]);

    /* intra warp reduction */
    {
        const unsigned int warp_activity = __ballot_sync(0xffffffff, block_is_active);
        if (laneid == 0) {
            block_count[warpid] = __popc(warp_activity);
        }
        __syncthreads();
    }

    /* intra block reduction and final atomic add */
    if (warpid == 0) {
        I active_block_count = (laneid >= warps_per_block ? I(0) : block_count[laneid]);

        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned int offset=warpsize/2u; offset>0u; offset>>=1u) {
            active_block_count += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, active_block_count, offset);
        }
        if (laneid == 0) {
            atomicAdd(active_blocks_count, active_block_count);
        }
    }
}


template<typename I, I Nthreads, unsigned int warpsize>
__launch_bounds__(Nthreads)
__global__ void compactActiveBlocks(
        const I Nblocks,
        const I allocated_active_blocks,
        const unsigned char *const __restrict block_mask,
        I *const __restrict compacted_block_indices,
        I *const __restrict active_blocks_count) {
    static_assert(Nthreads%warpsize==0, "Nthreads%warpsize != 0");
    static_assert(Nthreads/warpsize<=warpsize, "Nthreads/warpsize > warpsize");
    constexpr unsigned int warps_per_block = Nthreads/warpsize;

    __shared__ I warp_offset[warps_per_block+1];

    const unsigned int laneid = threadIdx.x % warpsize;
    const unsigned int warpid = threadIdx.x / warpsize;
    const I block_index = blockIdx.x*Nthreads + threadIdx.x;
    const bool block_is_active = (block_index >= Nblocks ? false : block_mask[block_index]);

    const I offset = prefix_sum<I, warps_per_block, warpsize>(
            block_is_active, laneid, warpid, warp_offset, active_blocks_count);

    if (block_is_active && (offset<allocated_active_blocks)) {
        compacted_block_indices[offset] = block_index;
    }
}


template <typename T, unsigned int dim>
struct BoundingBox {
    T a[dim], b[dim];
    __device__ bool empty() const {
        bool res = false;
        #pragma unroll dim
        for(int i=0; i < dim; ++i) {
            res |= (a[i] >= b[i]);
        }
        return res;
    }
};


} /* end of namespace cupmb */
```

### cupmb/kernels/pmb1D.cuh
```
/*
 * Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
 */

#ifndef PMB1D_CUH_JACGPBQT
#define PMB1D_CUH_JACGPBQT

#include "pmb.cuh"

/* The following code has been generated at runtime */
{{cupmb_func_definitions}}
{{cupmb_vertex_properties}}
/****************************************************/

namespace cupmb {

/* One-dimensional marching blocks: vertices are the zero crossings of the scalar field on the grid
 * edges [x_i, x_{i+1}] (linear interpolation) and each simplex is a single vertex (0-simplex), so that
 * vertices and simplices have shape (n,1) and simplex k references vertex k.
 * A grid edge belongs to exactly one logical block (the last thread of a block does not generate any
 * vertex), hence vertices are never duplicated on block boundaries.
 * 0-simplices carry no orientation: the normal (negative to positive scalar values) is the sign of
 * f(x_{i+1}) - f(x_i), it can be computed with a vertex property if required. */


template <typename T, typename I, I Bx, unsigned int warpsize=32, typename... F>
__launch_bounds__(Bx)
__global__ void computeBlockMask1D(
        const T x0, const T dx,
        const I Nx, /* cells in the grid (number of points - 1) */
        unsigned char *const __restrict block_mask,
        T *const __restrict output_scalar_field,
        F... fargs) {

    static_assert(is_same<T, float>::value || is_same<T, double>::value,
            "T has to be float or double.");
    static_assert(is_same<I, unsigned int>::value || is_same<I, unsigned long long>::value,
            "I has to be unsigned int or unsigned long long.");

    constexpr I warps_per_block = Bx/warpsize;
    static_assert(Bx%warpsize==0, "Bx%warpsize != 0");
    static_assert(Bx/warpsize<=warpsize, "Bx/warpsize > warpsize (in practice Bx <= warpsize**2 = 32**2 = 1024)");

    __shared__ T minbuf[warps_per_block], maxbuf[warps_per_block];

    const unsigned int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned int laneid = tid % warpsize;
    const unsigned int warpid = tid / warpsize;

    const I bid = blockIdx.x;
    const trove::array<I,1> idx{bid*(Bx-1) + tid};
    const I& ix = trove::get<0>(idx);

    const trove::array<T,1> pos{x0 + ix*dx};

    const bool inside_domain = (ix <= Nx);
    const T v = (inside_domain ? cupmb::scalar<T,I,1>(pos, idx, fargs...) : quiet_nan<T>());

    if (inside_domain && (output_scalar_field != nullptr)) {
        /* 64 bit indexing for consistency with 2D and 3D */
        using ull = unsigned long long;
        output_scalar_field[ull(ix)] = v;
    }

    /* out of domain values are NaN (ignored), inside domain NaN values are considered positive (see mask_value) */
    compute_block_mask<T, I, warps_per_block, warpsize>((inside_domain ? mask_value(v) : v),
            bid, laneid, warpid, minbuf, maxbuf, block_mask);
}


template <typename T, typename I,
    I Nthreads,
    I Bx, /* threads per logical block for simplex extraction (next step) */
    unsigned int warpsize=32, typename... F>
__launch_bounds__(Nthreads)
__global__ void compactActiveBlocksFromPredicate1D(
        const T x0,
        const T dx,
        const I Nx, /* cells in the grid (number of points - 1) */
        const I lx, /* current level of splitting */
        const I sx, /* new splits to be generated, this is equal to 0 only at start_level */
        const I current_active_blocks, /* number of input blocks, or upper bound when input_count is not null */
        const I *const __restrict input_count, /* number of input blocks computed on the device (can be null) */
        const I allocated_output_index_count,
        const I *const __restrict input_block_indices,
              I *const __restrict output_block_indices,
              I *const __restrict active_blocks_count,
        F... fargs) {

    static_assert(is_same<T, float>::value || is_same<T, double>::value,
            "T has to be float or double.");
    static_assert(is_same<I, unsigned int>::value || is_same<I, unsigned long long>::value,
            "I has to be unsigned int or unsigned long long.");

    const unsigned int laneid = threadIdx.x % warpsize;
    const unsigned int warpid = threadIdx.x / warpsize;
    const I tid = blockIdx.x*Nthreads + threadIdx.x;
    const I input_blocks = (input_count == nullptr ? current_active_blocks : min(current_active_blocks, *input_count));
    if (I(blockIdx.x)*Nthreads >= input_blocks) {
        /* the whole thread block is idle */
        return;
    }
    const bool thread_is_idle = (tid >= input_blocks);

    constexpr I warps_per_block = Nthreads/warpsize;
    static_assert(Nthreads%warpsize==0, "Nthreads%warpsize != 0");
    static_assert(Nthreads/warpsize<=warpsize, "Nthreads/warpsize > warpsize (in practice Nthreads <= warpsize**2 = 32**2 = 1024)");
    __shared__ I warp_offset[warps_per_block+1];

    /* number of final logical blocks in the grid */
    const I Gx = number_of_blocks(Nx, Bx);

    /* end level E corresponds to log2(H) with H = next_pow2(G) */
    const I Ex = log_of_next_pow2(Gx);

    /* level after this step, note that p = l at start_level because s=0 */
    const I px = lx+sx; assert(px <= Ex);

    const bool is_first_level = (sx == 0);

    /* get current virtual block index (binary tree node at level lx) */
    const I bx = (thread_is_idle ? I(0) : (is_first_level ? tid : input_block_indices[tid]));

    /* subblock size in number of cells (a subblock at level p contains 2**(E-p) final blocks) */
    const I cells_x = (Bx-1) << (Ex-px);

    /* Iterate on subblocks. Emptiness is tested with integers and bounds are computed with the same
     * formula as sample points (x0 + ix*dx) to avoid rounding issues (duplicated or missing blocks).
     * In 1D the subblock index is the same at intermediate and last levels (nbx < Gx at last level).
     * Bounding box axis follows scalar(x) argument: a[0]/b[0] = x. */
    BoundingBox<T,1> domain;
    #pragma unroll 2
    for(I kx=0; kx<(I(1)<<sx); ++kx) {
        const I nbx = (bx << sx) + kx;
        const I iax = nbx*cells_x;
        domain.a[0] = x0 + min(iax,           Nx)*dx;
        domain.b[0] = x0 + min(iax + cells_x, Nx)*dx;
        const bool empty = (iax >= Nx);
        const bool active = (thread_is_idle || empty ? false : may_contain_zeros<T, I, 1, F...>(domain, fargs...));
        const I offset = prefix_sum<I, warps_per_block, warpsize>(
                I(active), laneid, warpid, warp_offset, active_blocks_count);
        if (active && offset < allocated_output_index_count) {
            output_block_indices[offset] = nbx;
        }
    }
}


template <typename T, typename I, I Bx,
    unsigned int warpsize=32, typename... F>
__launch_bounds__(Bx)
__global__ void computeSimplices1D(
        const I allocated_vertex_count, const I allocated_simplex_count,
        const T x0,
        const T dx,
        const I Nx, /* cells in the grid (number of points - 1) */
        const I *const __restrict compacted_block_indices,
        const I *const __restrict active_block_count, /* number of active blocks computed on the device (can be null) */
        trove::array<T,1> *const __restrict output_vertices,
        trove::array<I,1> *const __restrict output_simplices,
        I *const __restrict output_vertex_and_simplex_count,
        const F... fargs) {

    static_assert(is_same<T,float>::value || is_same<T,double>::value,
            "T has to be float or double.");
    static_assert(is_same<I,unsigned int>::value || is_same<I,unsigned long long int>::value,
            "I has to be unsigned int or unsigned long long int.");
    using II = typename I2<I>::type;

    /* the grid may be larger than the number of active blocks when it is only known on the device */
    if ((active_block_count != nullptr) && (I(blockIdx.x) >= *active_block_count)) {
        return;
    }

    constexpr I warps_per_block = Bx/warpsize;
    static_assert(Bx%warpsize==0, "Bx%warpsize != 0");
    static_assert(Bx/warpsize<=warpsize, "Bx/warpsize > warpsize (in practice Bx <= warpsize**2 = 32**2 = 1024)");

    /* shared memory is allocated to share data of various types accross threads
     *  1) Bx*sizeof(T) shared_scalars
     *  2) (warps_per_block+1)*sizeof(II) = 2*(Bx/warpsize+1)*sizeof(I) shared_offsets
     * User should ensure to allocate at least the max of those two values. */
    extern __shared__ __align__(16) unsigned char shared_mem_buffer[];

    T  *const __restrict shared_scalars = reinterpret_cast<T*>(shared_mem_buffer);
    II *const __restrict shared_offsets = reinterpret_cast<II*>(shared_mem_buffer);

    const unsigned int tid = threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned int laneid = tid % warpsize;
    const unsigned int warpid = tid / warpsize;

    /* compute grid point position */
    trove::array<I,1> idx;
    trove::array<T,1> pos;
    I &ix(trove::get<0>(idx));
    T  &x(trove::get<0>(pos));
    {
        const I bid = compacted_block_indices[blockIdx.x];
        ix = bid*(Bx-1) + tid;
        x = x0 + ix*dx;
    }

    /* domain boundary test */
    const bool nx = (ix == Nx);
    const bool inside_domain = (ix <= Nx);

    /* thread block boundary test */
    const bool bx = (tid == Bx-1);

    /* load scalars */
    T s[2];
    {
        const trove::array<I,1>& cidx = idx;
        const trove::array<T,1>& cpos = pos;

        /* the user function is never evaluated outside of [0,N] */
        s[0] = (inside_domain ? cupmb::scalar<T,I,1>(cpos, cidx, fargs...) : T(1));

        shared_scalars[tid] = s[0];
        __syncthreads();

        /* Out-of-block neighbors are not evaluated: block boundary threads never generate vertices. */
        s[1] = (bx ? s[0] : shared_scalars[tid+1]);
        __syncthreads();
    }

    /* vertex (and simplex) generation on edge [x_i, x_{i+1}], NaN scalars are considered positive */
    const bool genvertex = inside_domain && !bx && !nx && ((s[0]<=T(0)) != (s[1]<=T(0)));

    /* Prefix scan output vertex id, both vertex and simplex counters are incremented by the same amount.
     * The two global counters are updated by two independent atomic operations, so the simplex offset
     * of a block may differ from its vertex offset: the vertex offset is used for both outputs, simplex k
     * is stored at index k and references vertex k. */
    II offsets;
    const I& vertex_offset = offsets.x;
    {
        II icounts;
        icounts.x = I(genvertex);
        icounts.y = I(genvertex);
        offsets = prefix_sum<II, warps_per_block, warpsize>(
                icounts, laneid, warpid, shared_offsets,
                reinterpret_cast<II*>(output_vertex_and_simplex_count));
    }

    /* store vertex and simplex in global memory */
    if (genvertex) {
        if (vertex_offset < allocated_vertex_count) {
            /* NaN scalars never produce NaN vertices (see edge_interpolation_parameter) */
            const T vertex = x + edge_interpolation_parameter(s[0], s[1])*dx;
            assert(!isnan(vertex));
            output_vertices[vertex_offset] = trove::make_array<T>(vertex);
        }
        if (vertex_offset < allocated_simplex_count) {
            output_simplices[vertex_offset] = trove::make_array<I>(vertex_offset);
        }
    }
}

} /* end of namespace cupmb */

#endif /* end of include guard: PMB1D_CUH_JACGPBQT */
```

### cupmb/kernels/pmb2D.cuh
```
/*
 * Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
 */

#ifndef PMB2D_CUH_MKPYXQV3
#define PMB2D_CUH_MKPYXQV3

#include "pmb.cuh"

/* The following code has been generated at runtime */
{{cupmb_func_definitions}}
{{cupmb_vertex_properties}}
/****************************************************/

namespace cupmb {

/* table that stores edges ids to be generated (up to nedges_to_generates)
 * Segments are oriented such that the negative region (s<=0) lies on their left: isolines are counterclockwise
 * around negative regions, the outward normal (dy,-dx) points towards positive values and the signed area
 * computed with the shoelace formula is the area of the negative region. */
__device__ __constant__ char4 pmb2d_case_table[16] = {
    {-1, -1, -1, -1},  // case 0
    {+0, +3, -1, -1},  // case 1
    {+1, +0, -1, -1},  // case 2
    {+1, +3, -1, -1},  // case 3
    {+2, +1, -1, -1},  // case 4
    {+0, +1, +2, +3},  // case 5
    {+2, +0, -1, -1},  // case 6
    {+2, +3, -1, -1},  // case 7
    {+3, +2, -1, -1},  // case 8
    {+0, +2, -1, -1},  // case 9
    {+3, +0, +1, +2},  // case 10
    {+1, +2, -1, -1},  // case 11
    {+3, +1, -1, -1},  // case 12
    {+0, +1, -1, -1},  // case 13
    {+3, +0, -1, -1},  // case 14
    {-1, -1, -1, -1},  // case 15
};


template <typename T, typename I, I Bx, I By, unsigned int warpsize=32, typename... F>
__launch_bounds__(Bx*By)
__global__ void computeBlockMask2D(
        const T x0, const T y0,
        const T dx, const T dy,
        const I Nx, const I Ny, /* cells in the grid (number of points - 1 in each direction) */
        unsigned char *const __restrict block_mask,
        T *const __restrict output_scalar_field,
        F... fargs) {

    static_assert(is_same<T, float>::value || is_same<T, double>::value,
            "T has to be float or double.");
    static_assert(is_same<I, unsigned int>::value || is_same<I, unsigned long long>::value,
            "I has to be unsigned int or unsigned long long.");

    constexpr I B = Bx*By;
    constexpr I warps_per_block = B/warpsize;
    static_assert(B%warpsize==0, "B%warpsize != 0");
    static_assert(B/warpsize<=warpsize, "B/warpsize > warpsize (in practice B <= warpsize**2 = 32**2 = 1024)");
    const I Gx = number_of_blocks(Nx, Bx);

    __shared__ T minbuf[warps_per_block], maxbuf[warps_per_block];

    const unsigned int tid = threadIdx.y*Bx + threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned int laneid = tid % warpsize;
    const unsigned int warpid = tid / warpsize;

    const I bid = blockIdx.y*Gx + blockIdx.x;
    const trove::array<I,2> idx{blockIdx.x*(Bx-1) + threadIdx.x,
                                blockIdx.y*(By-1) + threadIdx.y};
    const I& ix = trove::get<0>(idx);
    const I& iy = trove::get<1>(idx);

    const trove::array<T,2> pos{x0 + ix*dx,
                                y0 + iy*dy};

    const bool inside_domain = (ix <= Nx) && (iy <= Ny);
    const T v = (inside_domain ? cupmb::scalar<T,I,2>(pos, idx, fargs...) : quiet_nan<T>());

    if (inside_domain && (output_scalar_field != nullptr)) {
        /* x-fastest layout, 64 bit indexing as (Nx+1)*(Ny+1) may overflow I */
        using ull = unsigned long long;
        output_scalar_field[ull(iy)*ull(Nx+1) + ull(ix)] = v;
    }

    /* out of domain values are NaN (ignored), inside domain NaN values are considered positive (see mask_value) */
    compute_block_mask<T, I, warps_per_block, warpsize>((inside_domain ? mask_value(v) : v),
            bid, laneid, warpid, minbuf, maxbuf, block_mask);
}


template <typename T, typename I,
    I Nthreads,
    I Bx, I By, /* threads per logical block for simplex extraction (next step) */
    unsigned int warpsize=32, typename... F>
__launch_bounds__(Nthreads)
__global__ void compactActiveBlocksFromPredicate2D(
        const T x0, const T y0,
        const T dx, const T dy,
        const I Nx, const I Ny, /* cells in the grid (number of points - 1 in each direction) */
        const I lx, const I ly, /* current level of splitting */
        const I sx, const I sy, /* new splits to be generated, this is equal to (0,0) only at start_level */
        const I current_active_blocks, /* number of input blocks, or upper bound when input_count is not null */
        const I *const __restrict input_count, /* number of input blocks computed on the device (can be null) */
        const I allocated_output_index_count,
        const I *const __restrict input_block_indices,
              I *const __restrict output_block_indices,
              I *const __restrict active_blocks_count,
        F... fargs) {

    static_assert(is_same<T, float>::value || is_same<T, double>::value,
            "T has to be float or double.");
    static_assert(is_same<I, unsigned int>::value || is_same<I, unsigned long long>::value,
            "I has to be unsigned int or unsigned long long.");

    const unsigned int laneid = threadIdx.x % warpsize;
    const unsigned int warpid = threadIdx.x / warpsize;
    const I tid = blockIdx.x*Nthreads + threadIdx.x;
    const I input_blocks = (input_count == nullptr ? current_active_blocks : min(current_active_blocks, *input_count));
    if (I(blockIdx.x)*Nthreads >= input_blocks) {
        /* the whole thread block is idle */
        return;
    }
    const bool thread_is_idle = (tid >= input_blocks);

    constexpr I warps_per_block = Nthreads/warpsize;
    static_assert(Nthreads%warpsize==0, "B%warpsize != 0");
    static_assert(Nthreads/warpsize<=warpsize, "B/warpsize > warpsize (in practice B <= warpsize**2 = 32**2 = 1024)");
    __shared__ I warp_offset[warps_per_block+1];

    /* number of final logical blocks in the grid */
    const I Gx = number_of_blocks(Nx, Bx);
    const I Gy = number_of_blocks(Ny, By);

    /* end level E corresponds to log2(H) with H = next_pow2(G) */
    const I Ex = log_of_next_pow2(Gx);
    const I Ey = log_of_next_pow2(Gy);

    /* level after this step, note that p = l at start_level because s=0 */
    const I px = lx+sx; assert(px <= Ex);
    const I py = ly+sy; assert(py <= Ey);

    const bool is_first_level = (sx == 0) && (sy == 0);
    const bool is_last_level  = (px == Ex) && (py == Ey);

    /* get current virtual block index */
    const I bid = (thread_is_idle ? I(0) : (is_first_level ? tid : input_block_indices[tid]));
    I bx, by;
    {
        const I Hx = I(1) << lx;
        by = bid / Hx;
        bx = bid % Hx;
    }

    /* number of final virtual blocks in the current encompassing grid and next one */
    const I Ix = I(1) << px;

    /* subblock size in number of cells (a subblock at level p contains 2**(E-p) final blocks per direction) */
    const I cells_x = (Bx-1) << (Ex-px);
    const I cells_y = (By-1) << (Ey-py);

    /* Iterate on subblocks. Emptiness is tested with integers and bounds are computed with the same
     * formula as sample points (x0 + ix*dx) to avoid rounding issues (duplicated or missing blocks).
     * Bounding box axes follow scalar(x,y) argument order: a[0]/b[0] = x, a[1]/b[1] = y. */
    BoundingBox<T,2> domain;
    #pragma unroll 2
    for(I ky=0; ky<(I(1)<<sy); ++ky) {
        const I nby = (by << sy) + ky;
        const I iay = nby*cells_y;
        domain.a[1] = y0 + min(iay,           Ny)*dy;
        domain.b[1] = y0 + min(iay + cells_y, Ny)*dy;
        #pragma unroll 2
        for(I kx=0; kx<(I(1)<<sx); ++kx) {
            const I nbx = (bx << sx) + kx;
            const I iax = nbx*cells_x;
            const I subblock_index = (is_last_level ? nby*Gx + nbx : nby*Ix + nbx);
            domain.a[0] = x0 + min(iax,           Nx)*dx;
            domain.b[0] = x0 + min(iax + cells_x, Nx)*dx;
            const bool empty = (iax >= Nx) || (iay >= Ny);
            const bool active = (thread_is_idle || empty ? false : may_contain_zeros<T, I, 2, F...>(domain, fargs...));
            const I offset = prefix_sum<I, warps_per_block, warpsize>(
                    I(active), laneid, warpid, warp_offset, active_blocks_count);
            if (active && offset < allocated_output_index_count) {
                output_block_indices[offset] = subblock_index;
            }
        }
    }
}


template <typename T, typename I, I Bx, I By,
    unsigned int warpsize=32, typename... F>
__launch_bounds__(Bx*By)
__global__ void computeSimplices2D(
        const I allocated_vertex_count, const I allocated_edge_count,
        const T x0, const T y0,
        const T dx, const T dy,
        const I Nx, const I Ny, /* cells in the grid (number of points - 1 in each direction) */
        const I *const __restrict compacted_block_indices,
        const I *const __restrict active_block_count, /* number of active blocks computed on the device (can be null) */
        trove::array<T,2> *const __restrict output_vertices,
        trove::array<I,2> *const __restrict output_edges,
        I *const __restrict output_vertex_and_edge_count,
        const F... fargs) {

    static_assert(is_same<T,float>::value || is_same<T,double>::value,
            "T has to be float or double.");
    static_assert(is_same<I,unsigned int>::value || is_same<I,unsigned long long int>::value,
            "I has to be unsigned int or unsigned long long int.");
    using II = typename I2<I>::type;

    /* the grid may be larger than the number of active blocks when it is only known on the device */
    if ((active_block_count != nullptr) && (I(blockIdx.x) >= *active_block_count)) {
        return;
    }

    constexpr I B = Bx*By;
    constexpr I warps_per_block = B/warpsize;
    static_assert(B%warpsize==0, "B%warpsize != 0");
    static_assert(B/warpsize<=warpsize, "B/warpsize > warpsize (in practice B <= warpsize**2 = 32**2 = 1024)");
    const I Gx = number_of_blocks(Nx, Bx);

    /* shared memory is allocated to share data of various types accross threads
     *  1) B*sizeof(T) shared_scalars
     *  2) B*(sizeof(I)+sizeof(unsigned char)) shared_vertex_offsets and shared_vertex_count
     *  3) (warps_per_block+1)*sizeof(II) = 2*(Bx*By/warpsize)*sizeof(I) shared_offsets
     * User should ensure to allocate at least the max of those three values. */
    extern __shared__ __align__(16) unsigned char shared_mem_buffer[];

    T  *const __restrict shared_scalars = reinterpret_cast<T*>(shared_mem_buffer);
    II *const __restrict shared_offsets = reinterpret_cast<II*>(shared_mem_buffer);
    I  *const __restrict shared_vertex_offset = reinterpret_cast<I*>(shared_mem_buffer);
    unsigned char* const __restrict shared_vertex_count = reinterpret_cast<unsigned char*>(shared_vertex_offset + B);

    const unsigned int tx = threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned int ty = threadIdx.y;
    const unsigned int tid = ty*Bx + tx;
    const unsigned int laneid = tid % warpsize;
    const unsigned int warpid = tid / warpsize;

    /* compute vertex position */
    trove::array<I,2> idx;
    trove::array<T,2> pos;
    I &ix(trove::get<0>(idx)), &iy(trove::get<1>(idx));
    T  &x(trove::get<0>(pos)),  &y(trove::get<1>(pos));
    {
        I bid = compacted_block_indices[blockIdx.x];

        const I bidx = bid%Gx;
        const I bidy = bid/Gx;

        ix = bidx*(Bx-1) + tx;
        iy = bidy*(By-1) + ty;

        x = x0 + ix*dx;
        y = y0 + iy*dy;
    }

    /* domain boundary test */
    const bool nx = (ix == Nx);
    const bool ny = (iy == Ny);
    const bool nn = nx || ny;
    const bool inside_domain = (ix <= Nx) && (iy <= Ny);

    /* thread block boundary test */
    const bool bx = (tx == Bx-1);
    const bool by = (ty == By-1);
    const bool bb = bx || by;

    /* load scalars and compute case id */
    T s[4];
    unsigned char case_id = 0u;
    {
        const trove::array<I,2>& cidx = idx;
        const trove::array<T,2>& cpos = pos;

        /* the user function is never evaluated outside of [0,N] */
        s[0] = (inside_domain ? cupmb::scalar<T,I,2>(cpos, cidx, fargs...) : T(1));

        shared_scalars[tid] = s[0];
        __syncthreads();

        /* Out-of-block neighbors are not evaluated: block boundary threads never generate edges and vertex
         * generation only depends on edges 0 and 3 (s[1] and s[3]), which are masked by bx and by respectively.
         * Setting them to s[0] keeps the case id consistent. */
        s[1] = (bx ? s[0] : shared_scalars[tid+0*Bx+1]);
        s[2] = (bb ? s[0] : shared_scalars[tid+1*Bx+1]);
        s[3] = (by ? s[0] : shared_scalars[tid+1*Bx+0]);
        __syncthreads();

        #pragma unroll 4
        for (unsigned int k = 0u; k < 4u; ++k) {
            case_id += (s[k]<=T(0)) << k;
        }
    }

    /* load or compute edge table, vertex count, edge count and vertex generation */
    char4 edges = pmb2d_case_table[case_id];
    const char *const __restrict edges_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&edges);

    unsigned char nedges(0), nvertices(0);
    bool genvertex[2] = {false, false};

    #pragma unroll 4
    for (unsigned int k=0u; k<4u; k++) {
        if (edges_ptr[k] == -1) { break; }
        genvertex[0] |= (edges_ptr[k]==0);
        genvertex[1] |= (edges_ptr[k]==3);
        nedges += (k==0 || k==2);
    }
    const bool can_generate_edges = inside_domain && !bb && !nn;
    nedges = (can_generate_edges ? nedges : 0);

    genvertex[0] &= inside_domain && !bx && !nx;
    genvertex[1] &= inside_domain && !by && !ny;
    nvertices = genvertex[0] + genvertex[1];

    /* prefix scan output vertex id and output edge id */
    II offsets;
    I& vertex_offset = offsets.x;
    I&   edge_offset = offsets.y;
    {
        II icounts;
        icounts.x = nvertices;
        icounts.y = nedges;
        offsets = prefix_sum<II, warps_per_block, warpsize>(
                icounts, laneid, warpid, shared_offsets,
                reinterpret_cast<II*>(output_vertex_and_edge_count));
        shared_vertex_offset[tid] = vertex_offset;
        shared_vertex_count[tid] = nvertices;
        __syncthreads();
    }

    /* compute thread-local vertices (at most two out of four) and store them in global memory */
    {
        #pragma unroll 2
        for (unsigned int k = 0u; (k<2u) && (vertex_offset < allocated_vertex_count); ++k) {
            T vertex[2];
            if (genvertex[k]) {
                /* NaN scalars are considered positive and never produce NaN vertices (see edge_interpolation_parameter) */
                vertex[0] = x + (k==0 ? edge_interpolation_parameter(s[0], s[1])*dx : T(0));
                vertex[1] = y + (k==1 ? edge_interpolation_parameter(s[0], s[3])*dy : T(0));
                assert(!isnan(vertex[0]));
                assert(!isnan(vertex[1]));
                output_vertices[vertex_offset] = trove::make_array<T>(vertex[0], vertex[1]);
                ++vertex_offset;
            }
        }
    }

    /* compute edge vertex indices (at most two edges, representing four indices) and store them to global memory */
    I vidx;
    #pragma unroll 2
    for (unsigned int k = 0u; (k<nedges) && (edge_offset < allocated_edge_count); ++k) {
        I simplex[2];
        #pragma unroll 2
        for(unsigned int i = 0u; i<2; ++i) {
            const char e = edges_ptr[2*k+i];
            const int neighbor_tid = tid + (e==2)*Bx + (e==1);
            vidx = shared_vertex_offset[neighbor_tid] + (e==1 || e==3)*(shared_vertex_count[neighbor_tid]==2);
            simplex[i] = min(vidx, allocated_vertex_count-1);
        }
        output_edges[edge_offset] = trove::make_array<I>(simplex[0], simplex[1]);
        ++edge_offset;
    }
}

} /* end of namespace cupmb */

#endif /* end of include guard: PMB2D_CUH_MKPYXQV3 */
```

### cupmb/kernels/pmb3D.cuh

{{cupmb_func_definitions}}

namespace cupmb {

/* Table that stores vertices and triangles to be generated (at most 5 triangles -- 15 vertex indices/case).  */
/* Each of the 12 cube edge is reprented by 4 bit in a 64 bit ulonglong (60 bits total, from bit 4 to bit 63) */
/* Cube edges are indexed from 0 to 11 included, missing/unused edges are represented by the value 15 = 0xf.  */
/* Bit 0 corresponds to generate vertex on x edge, bit 1 to generate vertex on y edge and bit 2 to generate   */
/* vertex on z edge. Bit 3 is not used and is set to zero. The number of vertices to generate locally can be  */
/* deduced by computing the sum of vertex generation bits. The number of triangles to generate can be deduced */
/* efficiently by counting the number of leading (most significant) bits that are set to 1, divided by 12.    */
__device__ __constant__ unsigned long long pmb3d_case_table[256] = {
    0xfffffffffffffff0ull, 0xffffffffffff3807ull, 0xffffffffffff9101ull, 0xfffffffff1893816ull, 0xffffffffffffa210ull, 0xfffffffffa213807ull, 0xfffffffff920a291ull, 0xffffff89a8a23826ull,
    0xffffffffffff2b32ull, 0xfffffffff0b82b05ull, 0xfffffffffb320913ull, 0xffffffb89b912b14ull, 0xfffffffff3ab1a32ull, 0xffffffab8a801a05ull, 0xffffff9ab9b30933ull, 0xfffffffffb8aa894ull,
    0xffffffffffff8744ull, 0xfffffffff4370343ull, 0xfffffffff7489105ull, 0xffffff1371749142ull, 0xfffffffff748a214ull, 0xffffffa214037433ull, 0xffffff748209a295ull, 0xfff4973727929a22ull,
    0xfffffffff2b37486ull, 0xffffff40242b74b1ull, 0xffffffb327481097ull, 0xfff1292b9b49b740ull, 0xffffff487ab31a36ull, 0xfff4b7401b41ab11ull, 0xfff30bab9b098747ull, 0xffffffab99b4b740ull,

__device__ __constant__ char4 pmb3d_offset_table[12] {
    char4{0, 0, 0, 0}, /* e0 */
    char4{1, 0, 0, 1}, /* e1 */
    char4{0, 1, 0, 0}, /* e2 */
    char4{0, 0, 0, 1}, /* e3 */

    char4{0, 0, 1, 0}, /* e4 */
    char4{1, 0, 1, 1}, /* e5 */
    char4{0, 1, 1, 0}, /* e6 */
    char4{0, 0, 1, 1}, /* e7 */

    char4{0, 0, 0, 2}, /* e8 */
    char4{1, 0, 0, 2}, /* e9 */
    char4{1, 1, 0, 2}, /* e10 */
    char4{0, 1, 0, 2}, /* e11 */
};


class Triangle {
    public:
        __device__ __forceinline__ Triangle() : _value() {}
        __device__ __forceinline__ Triangle(unsigned long long value): _value(value) {}
        __device__ __forceinline__ void set_value(unsigned long long value) { this->_value = value; }
        __device__ __forceinline__ unsigned char operator[](unsigned int i) const {
            return (unsigned char)( (this->_value >> ((i+1) << 2)) & 0xf);
        }
        __device__ __forceinline__ void determine(unsigned char *const __restrict genvertex,
                unsigned char& nvertices, unsigned char& ntriangles,
                const bool bxx, const bool byy, const bool bzz,
                const bool nxx, const bool nyy, const bool nzz,
                const bool inside_domain) const {
            /* vertices cannot be generated outside domain or at block/grid boundary */
            genvertex[0] = inside_domain && !bxx && !nxx;
            genvertex[1] = inside_domain && !byy && !nyy;
            genvertex[2] = inside_domain && !bzz && !nzz;
            const bool can_generate_triangles = genvertex[0] && genvertex[1] && genvertex[2];
            genvertex[0] &= (this->_value >> 0) & 0x1;
            genvertex[1] &= (this->_value >> 1) & 0x1;
            genvertex[2] &= (this->_value >> 2) & 0x1;
            /* bit 3 left unused */
            nvertices  = genvertex[0] + genvertex[1] + genvertex[2];
            ntriangles = (can_generate_triangles ? 5 - __clzll(~this->_value)/12 : 0);
        }
    private:
        unsigned long long _value;
};


template <typename T, typename I, I Bx, I By, I Bz, unsigned int warpsize=32, typename... F>
__launch_bounds__(Bx*By*Bz)
__global__ void computeBlockMask3D(
        const T x0, const T y0, const T z0,
        const T dx, const T dy, const T dz,
        const I Nx, const I Ny, const I Nz, /* cuboids in the grid (number of points - 1 in each direction) */
        unsigned char *const __restrict block_mask,
        T *const __restrict output_scalar_field,
        F... fargs) {

    static_assert(is_same<T, float>::value || is_same<T, double>::value,
            "T has to be float or double.");
    static_assert(is_same<I, unsigned int>::value || is_same<I, unsigned long long>::value,
            "I has to be unsigned int or unsigned long long.");

    constexpr I B = Bx*By*Bz;
    constexpr I warps_per_block = B/warpsize;
    static_assert(B%warpsize==0, "B%warpsize != 0");
    static_assert(B/warpsize<=warpsize, "B/warpsize > warpsize (in practice B <= warpsize**2 = 32**2 = 1024)");
    const I Gx = number_of_blocks(Nx, Bx);
    const I Gy = number_of_blocks(Ny, By);

    __shared__ T minbuf[warps_per_block], maxbuf[warps_per_block];

    const unsigned int tid = (threadIdx.z*By + threadIdx.y)*Bx + threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned int laneid = tid % warpsize;
    const unsigned int warpid = tid / warpsize;

    const I bid = (blockIdx.z*Gy + blockIdx.y)*Gx + blockIdx.x;
    const trove::array<I,3> idx = trove::make_array<I>(blockIdx.x*(Bx-1) + threadIdx.x,
                                                       blockIdx.y*(By-1) + threadIdx.y,
                                                       blockIdx.z*(Bz-1) + threadIdx.z);
    const I& ix = trove::get<0>(idx);
    const I& iy = trove::get<1>(idx);
    const I& iz = trove::get<2>(idx);

    const trove::array<T,3> pos = trove::make_array<T>(x0 + ix*dx,
                                                       y0 + iy*dy,
                                                       z0 + iz*dz);

    const bool inside_domain = (ix <= Nx) && (iy <= Ny) && (iz <= Nz);
    const T v = (inside_domain ? cupmb::scalar<T,I,3>(pos, idx, fargs...) : quiet_nan<T>());

    if (inside_domain && (output_scalar_field != nullptr)) {
        /* x-fastest layout, 64 bit indexing as (Nx+1)*(Ny+1)*(Nz+1) may overflow I */
        using ull = unsigned long long;
        output_scalar_field[(ull(iz)*ull(Ny+1) + ull(iy))*ull(Nx+1) + ull(ix)] = v;
    }

    /* out of domain values are NaN (ignored), inside domain NaN values are considered positive (see mask_value) */
    compute_block_mask<T, I, warps_per_block, warpsize>((inside_domain ? mask_value(v) : v),
            bid, laneid, warpid, minbuf, maxbuf, block_mask);
}



template <typename T, typename I,
    I Nthreads,
    I Bx, I By, I Bz, /* threads per logical block for simplex extraction (next step) */
    unsigned int warpsize=32, typename... F>
__launch_bounds__(Nthreads)
__global__ void compactActiveBlocksFromPredicate3D(
        const T x0, const T y0, const T z0,
        const T dx, const T dy, const T dz,
        const I Nx, const I Ny, const I Nz, /* cuboids in the grid (number of points - 1 in each direction) */
        const I lx, const I ly, const I lz, /* current level of splitting */
        const I sx, const I sy, const I sz, /* new splits to be generated, this is equal to (0,0,0) only at start_level */
        const I current_active_blocks, /* number of input blocks, or upper bound when input_count is not null */
        const I *const __restrict input_count, /* number of input blocks computed on the device (can be null) */
        const I allocated_output_index_count,
        const I *const __restrict input_block_indices,
              I *const __restrict output_block_indices,
              I *const __restrict active_blocks_count,
        F... fargs) {

    static_assert(is_same<T, float>::value || is_same<T, double>::value,
            "T has to be float or double.");
    static_assert(is_same<I, unsigned int>::value || is_same<I, unsigned long long>::value,
            "I has to be unsigned int or unsigned long long.");

    const unsigned int laneid = threadIdx.x % warpsize;
    const unsigned int warpid = threadIdx.x / warpsize;
    const I tid = blockIdx.x*Nthreads + threadIdx.x;
    const I input_blocks = (input_count == nullptr ? current_active_blocks : min(current_active_blocks, *input_count));
    if (I(blockIdx.x)*Nthreads >= input_blocks) {
        /* the whole thread block is idle */
        return;
    }
    const bool thread_is_idle = (tid >= input_blocks);

    constexpr I warps_per_block = Nthreads/warpsize;
    static_assert(Nthreads%warpsize==0, "B%warpsize != 0");
    static_assert(Nthreads/warpsize<=warpsize, "B/warpsize > warpsize (in practice B <= warpsize**2 = 32**2 = 1024)");
    __shared__ I warp_offset[warps_per_block+1];

    /* number of final logical blocks in the grid */
    const I Gx = number_of_blocks(Nx, Bx);
    const I Gy = number_of_blocks(Ny, By);
    const I Gz = number_of_blocks(Nz, Bz);

    /* end level E corresponds to log2(H) with H = next_pow2(G) */
    const I Ex = log_of_next_pow2(Gx);
    const I Ey = log_of_next_pow2(Gy);
    const I Ez = log_of_next_pow2(Gz);

    /* level after this step, note that p = l at start_level because s=0 */
    const I px = lx+sx; assert(px <= Ex);
    const I py = ly+sy; assert(py <= Ey);
    const I pz = lz+sz; assert(pz <= Ez);

    const bool is_first_level = (sx == 0) && (sy == 0) && (sz == 0);
    const bool is_last_level  = (px == Ex) && (py == Ey) && (pz == Ez);

    /* get current virtual block index */
    const I bid = (thread_is_idle ? I(0) : (is_first_level ? tid : input_block_indices[tid]));
    I bx, by, bz;
    {
        const I Hx = I(1) << lx;
        const I Hy = I(1) << ly;
        bz = bid / (Hx*Hy);
        by = (bid - bz * Hx * Hy) / Hx;
        bx = bid % Hx;
    }

    /* number of final virtual blocks in the current encompassing grid and next one */
    const I Ix = I(1) << px;
    const I Iy = I(1) << py;

    /* subblock size in number of cells (a subblock at level p contains 2**(E-p) final blocks per direction) */
    const I cells_x = (Bx-1) << (Ex-px);
    const I cells_y = (By-1) << (Ey-py);
    const I cells_z = (Bz-1) << (Ez-pz);

    /* Iterate on subblocks. Emptiness is tested with integers and bounds are computed with the same
     * formula as sample points (x0 + ix*dx) to avoid rounding issues (duplicated or missing blocks).
     * Bounding box axes follow scalar(x,y,z) argument order: a[0]/b[0] = x, a[1]/b[1] = y, a[2]/b[2] = z. */
    BoundingBox<T,3> domain;
    #pragma unroll 2
    for(I kz=0; kz<(I(1)<<sz); ++kz) {
        const I nbz = (bz << sz) + kz;
        const I iaz = nbz*cells_z;
        domain.a[2] = z0 + min(iaz,           Nz)*dz;
        domain.b[2] = z0 + min(iaz + cells_z, Nz)*dz;
        #pragma unroll 2
        for(I ky=0; ky<(I(1)<<sy); ++ky) {
            const I nby = (by << sy) + ky;
            const I iay = nby*cells_y;
            domain.a[1] = y0 + min(iay,           Ny)*dy;
            domain.b[1] = y0 + min(iay + cells_y, Ny)*dy;
            #pragma unroll 2
            for(I kx=0; kx<(I(1)<<sx); ++kx) {
                const I nbx = (bx << sx) + kx;
                const I iax = nbx*cells_x;
                const I subblock_index = (is_last_level ? (nbz*Gy + nby)*Gx + nbx : (nbz*Iy + nby)*Ix + nbx);
                domain.a[0] = x0 + min(iax,           Nx)*dx;
                domain.b[0] = x0 + min(iax + cells_x, Nx)*dx;
                const bool empty = (iax >= Nx) || (iay >= Ny) || (iaz >= Nz);
                const bool active = (thread_is_idle || empty ? false : may_contain_zeros<T, I, 3, F...>(domain, fargs...));
                const I offset = prefix_sum<I, warps_per_block, warpsize>(
                        I(active), laneid, warpid, warp_offset, active_blocks_count);
                if (active && offset < allocated_output_index_count) {
                    output_block_indices[offset] = subblock_index;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}


template <typename T, typename I, I Bx, I By, I Bz,
    unsigned int warpsize=32, bool use_constant_memory=true, typename... F>
__launch_bounds__(Bx*By*Bz)
__global__ void computeSimplices3D(
        const I allocated_vertex_count, const I allocated_triangle_count,
        const T x0, const T y0, const T z0,
        const T dx, const T dy, const T dz,
        const I Nx, const I Ny, const I Nz, /* cuboids in the grid (number of points - 1 in each direction) */
        const I *const __restrict compacted_block_indices,
        const I *const __restrict active_block_count, /* number of active blocks computed on the device (can be null) */
        trove::array<T,3> *const __restrict output_vertices,
        trove::array<I,3> *const __restrict output_triangles,
        I *const __restrict output_vertex_and_triangle_count,
        const F... fargs) {

    static_assert(is_same<T, float>::value || is_same<T, double>::value,
            "T has to be float or double.");
    static_assert(is_same<I, unsigned int>::value || is_same<I, unsigned long long>::value,
            "I has to be unsigned int or unsigned long long.");
    using II = typename I2<I>::type;

    /* the grid may be larger than the number of active blocks when it is only known on the device */
    if ((active_block_count != nullptr) && (I(blockIdx.x) >= *active_block_count)) {
        return;
    }

    constexpr I B = Bx*By*Bz;
    constexpr I warps_per_block = B/warpsize;
    static_assert(B%warpsize==0, "B%warpsize != 0");
    static_assert(B/warpsize<=warpsize, "B/warpsize > warpsize (in practice B <= warpsize**2 = 32**2 = 1024)");
    const I Gx = number_of_blocks(Nx, Bx);
    const I Gy = number_of_blocks(Ny, By);

    /* Shared memory is allocated to share data of various types accross threads
     *  1) B*sizeof(T) shared_scalars
     *  2) B*(sizeof(I)+sizeof(unsigned char)) shared_vertex_offsets and shared_local_offsets
     *  3) (warps_per_block+1)*sizeof(II) = 2*(Bx*By*Bz/warpsize)*sizeof(I) shared_offsets
     * Kernel caller should ensure to allocate at least the max of those three values. */
    extern __shared__ __align__(16) unsigned char shared_mem_buffer[];

    T  *const __restrict shared_scalars       = reinterpret_cast<T*>(shared_mem_buffer);
    II *const __restrict shared_offsets       = reinterpret_cast<II*>(shared_mem_buffer);
    I  *const __restrict shared_vertex_offset = reinterpret_cast<I*>(shared_mem_buffer);
    unsigned char* const __restrict shared_local_offsets = reinterpret_cast<unsigned char*>(shared_vertex_offset + B);

    const unsigned int tx = threadIdx.x;
    const unsigned int ty = threadIdx.y;
    const unsigned int tz = threadIdx.z;
    const unsigned int tid = (tz*By+ty)*Bx + tx;
    const unsigned int laneid = tid % warpsize;
    const unsigned int warpid = tid / warpsize;

    /* compute vertex position */
    trove::array<I,3> idx;
    trove::array<T,3> pos;
    I &ix(trove::get<0>(idx)), &iy(trove::get<1>(idx)), &iz(trove::get<2>(idx));
    T  &x(trove::get<0>(pos)),  &y(trove::get<1>(pos)),  &z(trove::get<2>(pos));
    {
        I bid = compacted_block_indices[blockIdx.x];

        const I bidz = bid/(Gx*Gy);
        bid -= bidz*Gx*Gy;
        const I bidy = bid/Gx;
        bid -= bidy*Gx;
        const I bidx = bid;

        ix = bidx*(Bx-1) + tx;
        iy = bidy*(By-1) + ty;
        iz = bidz*(Bz-1) + tz;

        x = x0 + ix*dx;
        y = y0 + iy*dy;
        z = z0 + iz*dz;
    }

    /* domain boundary test */
    const bool nxx = (ix == Nx);
    const bool nyy = (iy == Ny);
    const bool nzz = (iz == Nz);
    const bool inside_domain = (ix <= Nx) && (iy <= Ny) && (iz <= Nz);

    /* thread block boundary test */
    const bool bxx = (tx == Bx-1);
    const bool byy = (ty == By-1);
    const bool bzz = (tz == Bz-1);

    /* load scalars and compute case id */
    T s[8];
    unsigned char case_id = 0u;
    {
        const trove::array<I,3>& cidx = idx;
        const trove::array<T,3>& cpos = pos;
        const bool bxy = bxx || byy;
        const bool bxz = bxx || bzz;
        const bool byz = byy || bzz;
        const bool bbb = bxx || byy || bzz;

        /* the user function is never evaluated outside of [0,N] */
        s[0] = (inside_domain ? cupmb::scalar<T,I,3>(cpos, cidx, fargs...) : T(1));

        shared_scalars[tid] = s[0];
        __syncthreads();

        /* Out-of-block neighbors are not evaluated: block boundary threads never generate triangles and vertex
         * generation bits only depend on edges 0, 3 and 8 (s[1], s[3] and s[4]), which are masked by bxx, byy
         * and bzz respectively (see Triangle::determine). Setting them to s[0] keeps the case id consistent. */
        s[1] = (bxx ? s[0] : shared_scalars[tid+0*Bx*By+0*Bx+1]);
        s[2] = (bxy ? s[0] : shared_scalars[tid+0*Bx*By+1*Bx+1]);
        s[3] = (byy ? s[0] : shared_scalars[tid+0*Bx*By+1*Bx+0]);
        s[7] = (byz ? s[0] : shared_scalars[tid+1*Bx*By+1*Bx+0]);
        s[6] = (bbb ? s[0] : shared_scalars[tid+1*Bx*By+1*Bx+1]);
        s[5] = (bxz ? s[0] : shared_scalars[tid+1*Bx*By+0*Bx+1]);
        s[4] = (bzz ? s[0] : shared_scalars[tid+1*Bx*By+0*Bx+0]);
        __syncthreads();

        #pragma unroll 8
        for (unsigned int k = 0u; k < 8u; ++k) {
            case_id += (s[k]<=T(0)) << k;
        }
    }

    /* load triangle table and compute vertex count, triangle count and vertex generation mask */
    unsigned char nvertices, ntriangles, genvertex[3];
    Triangle triangles(pmb3d_case_table[case_id]);
    triangles.determine(genvertex, nvertices, ntriangles,
            bxx, byy, bzz, nxx, nyy, nzz, inside_domain);

    /* prefix scan vertex count and triangle count to obtain offsets in global memory */
    II offsets;
    I& vertex_offset   = offsets.x;
    I& triangle_offset = offsets.y;
    {
        II icounts;
        icounts.x = nvertices;
        icounts.y = ntriangles;
        offsets = prefix_sum<II, warps_per_block, warpsize>(
                icounts, laneid, warpid, shared_offsets,
                reinterpret_cast<II*>(output_vertex_and_triangle_count));
        shared_vertex_offset[tid] = vertex_offset;
        shared_local_offsets[tid] = 0 + (genvertex[0]<<2) + ((genvertex[0]+genvertex[1])<<4);
        /* x vertex offset -- y vertex offset -- z vertex offset -- 2 bits each, max offset = 2 */
        __syncthreads();
    }

    /* compute thread-local vertices (at most three out of twelve) and store them in global memory */
    #pragma unroll 3
    for (unsigned int k = 0u; (k<3u) && (vertex_offset < allocated_vertex_count); ++k) {
        T vertex[3];
        if (genvertex[k]) {
            /* NaN scalars are considered positive and never produce NaN vertices (see edge_interpolation_parameter) */
            vertex[0] = x + (k==0 ? edge_interpolation_parameter(s[0], s[1])*dx : T(0));
            vertex[1] = y + (k==1 ? edge_interpolation_parameter(s[0], s[3])*dy : T(0));
            vertex[2] = z + (k==2 ? edge_interpolation_parameter(s[0], s[4])*dz : T(0));
            assert(!isnan(vertex[0]));
            assert(!isnan(vertex[1]));
            assert(!isnan(vertex[2]));
            output_vertices[vertex_offset] = trove::make_array<T>(vertex[0], vertex[1], vertex[2]);
            ++vertex_offset;
        }
    }

    /* compute edge vertex indices (at most 5 triangles, representing 15 indices) and store them to global memory */
    #pragma unroll 5
    for (unsigned int k = 0u; (k<ntriangles) && (triangle_offset < allocated_triangle_count); ++k) {
        I simplex[3];
        #pragma unroll 3
        for(unsigned int i = 0u; i<3u; ++i) {
            const unsigned char e = triangles[3*k+i];
            unsigned char neighbor_vid;
            bool ox, oy, oz;
            if constexpr (use_constant_memory) {
                const char4 ot = pmb3d_offset_table[e];
                ox = ot.x;
                oy = ot.y;
                oz = ot.z;
                neighbor_vid = ot.w;
            }
            else {
                ox = (e==1) || (e==5) || (e==9)  || (e==10);
                oy = (e==2) || (e==6) || (e==10) || (e==11);
                oz = (e>=4) && (e<8);
                neighbor_vid = (e<8 ? e%2==1 : 2);
            }
            const unsigned int  neighbor_tid = tid + (oz*By+oy)*Bx+ox;
            const unsigned char voffset = (shared_local_offsets[neighbor_tid]>>(neighbor_vid<<1)) & 0x3;
            const I vertex_id = shared_vertex_offset[neighbor_tid] + voffset;
            simplex[i] = min(vertex_id, allocated_vertex_count-1);
        }
        /* The case table winding is clockwise when seen from the positive side, swap the last two indices so that
         * triangles are counterclockwise: the geometric normal (v1-v0)x(v2-v0) points from negative to positive
         * values (along the gradient) and closed surfaces have a positive signed volume. */
        output_triangles[triangle_offset] = trove::make_array<I>(simplex[0], simplex[2], simplex[1]);
        ++triangle_offset;
    }
}

} /* end of namespace cupmb */

#endif /* end of include guard: PMB3D_CUH_N9OLEPWY */
```

import os

import cusvna

module_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(os.path.join(__file__, "..")))
cuda_kernels_dir = os.path.join(module_dir, "kernels")
utils_dir = os.path.join(module_dir, "utils")
# cusvna headers (interval and affine arithmetic for block predicates) come from the cusvna package
cusvna_include_dir = cusvna.get_include()

if not os.path.exists(module_dir):
    msg = f"Cannot find module directory '{module_dir}'."
    raise RuntimeError(msg)

if not os.path.exists(utils_dir):
    msg = f"Cannot find utils directory '{utils_dir}'."
    raise RuntimeError(msg)

if not os.path.exists(cuda_kernels_dir):
    msg = f"Cannot find cuda directory '{cuda_kernels_dir}'."
    raise RuntimeError(msg)
