### cupmb/utils/kernel_cache.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
"""
On disk cache of the compiled kernels (cubin or ptx and mangled kernel names).

The cache directory is given by the CUPMB_CACHE_DIR environment variable and defaults to
$XDG_CACHE_HOME/cupmb (~/.cache/cupmb). The cache is disabled when CUPMB_DISABLE_KERNEL_CACHE
is set to a non empty value other than 0.

A cache entry is identified by the sha256 of the cupmb version, the backend and its compiler
version, the device compute capability, the kernel source code, the build options, the kernel
names and the content of all the files of the cupmb kernel directory (including the trove headers)
and of the cusvna headers. Headers included from other directories (for instance user headers given
through extra build options) are not part of the key, clear the cache when they change.
"""

import os, json, shutil, hashlib, tempfile, functools

from cupmb.utils.IO import cuda_kernels_dir, cusvna_include_dir

_CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION = 1


def kernel_cache_enabled():
    value = os.environ.get("CUPMB_DISABLE_KERNEL_CACHE", "").strip()
    return value in ("", "0")


def kernel_cache_dir():
    """Directory that contains the compiled kernels (it may not exist yet)."""
    cache_dir = os.environ.get("CUPMB_CACHE_DIR", "").strip()
    if not cache_dir:
        xdg_cache_home = os.environ.get("XDG_CACHE_HOME", "").strip() or os.path.join("~", ".cache")
        cache_dir = os.path.join(xdg_cache_home, "cupmb")
    return os.path.join(os.path.abspath(os.path.expanduser(cache_dir)), "kernels")


def clear_kernel_cache():
    """Remove all the compiled kernels from the on disk cache."""
    cache_dir = kernel_cache_dir()
    if os.path.isdir(cache_dir):
        shutil.rmtree(cache_dir)


@functools.cache
def _kernel_headers_digest():
    # computed once per process, symbolic links (trove) are followed
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for prefix, top in (("", cuda_kernels_dir), ("cusvna", os.path.join(cusvna_include_dir, "cusvna"))):
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(top, followlinks=True):
            dirs.sort()
            for filename in sorted(files):
                path = os.path.join(root, filename)
                h.update(os.path.join(prefix, os.path.relpath(path, top)).encode())
                h.update(b"\0")
                with open(path, "rb") as f:
                    h.update(hashlib.sha256(f.read()).digest())
    return h.hexdigest()


def kernel_cache_key(backend, compiler_version, device_major, device_minor, source_code, kernel_names, build_options):
    from cupmb import __version__

    key = {
        "format": _CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION,
        "cupmb": __version__,
        "backend": backend,
        "compiler": compiler_version,
        "arch": [int(device_major), int(device_minor)],
        "headers": _kernel_headers_digest(),
        "build_options": list(build_options),
        "kernel_names": list(kernel_names),
        "source": hashlib.sha256(source_code.encode()).hexdigest(),
    }
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(key, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()


def _entry_paths(key):
    cache_dir = kernel_cache_dir()
    return os.path.join(cache_dir, f"{key}.bin"), os.path.join(cache_dir, f"{key}.json")


def load_kernel_cache_entry(key):
    """
    Return the path of the cached binary and the mangled kernel names, or None if the entry
    does not exist or is corrupted.
    """
    binary_path, metadata_path = _entry_paths(key)
    try:
        with open(metadata_path) as f:
            metadata = json.load(f)
        with open(binary_path, "rb") as f:
            binary = f.read()
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None
    if metadata.get("sha256") != hashlib.sha256(binary).hexdigest():
        return None
    return binary_path, tuple(metadata["mangled_kernel_names"])


def _atomic_write(path, data, mode):
    fd, tmp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=os.path.dirname(path), prefix=".tmp_")
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, mode) as f:
            f.write(data)
        os.replace(tmp_path, path)
    except BaseException:
        os.unlink(tmp_path)
        raise


def store_kernel_cache_entry(key, binary, mangled_kernel_names):
    """
    Store a compiled module, return the path of the cached binary. Concurrent writers are
    safe: files are written atomically and the metadata (written last) validates the binary.
    """
    binary_path, metadata_path = _entry_paths(key)
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(binary_path), exist_ok=True)
    if isinstance(binary, str):
        binary = binary.encode()
    metadata = {
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(binary).hexdigest(),
        "mangled_kernel_names": list(mangled_kernel_names),
    }
    _atomic_write(binary_path, binary, "wb")
    _atomic_write(metadata_path, json.dumps(metadata), "w")
    return binary_path
```

### cupmb/utils/logger.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
import os, copy, logging, functools

from colorama import init as init_colorama
from termcolor import colored

init_colorama()


class ColoredFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    colored_levels = {
        "DEBUG": dict(color="white", attrs=["bold"]),
        "VERBOSE": dict(color="blue", attrs=["bold"]),
        "INFO": dict(color="green", attrs=["bold"]),
        "WARNING": dict(color="yellow", attrs=["bold"]),
        "ERROR": dict(color="red", attrs=["bold"]),
        "CRITICAL": dict(color="red", attrs=["bold", "underline"]),
    }

    def __init__(self, fmt, use_color=True):
        super().__init__(fmt)
        self.use_color = use_color

    def format(self, record):
        levelname = record.levelname
        if self.use_color and levelname in self.colored_levels:
            # Work on a copy so that other handlers see the original record, and merge
            # %-style arguments before coloring so that they are never lost.
            record = copy.copy(record)
            record.msg = colored(record.getMessage(), **self.colored_levels[levelname])
            record.args = None
        return super().format(record)


_FORMAT = " ".join(
    [
        colored("%(asctime)s", "green", attrs=["bold"]),
        colored("%(name)s", "blue", attrs=["bold"]),
        colored("%(levelname)5s", "white", attrs=["bold", "dark"]),
        "%(message)s",
    ]
)

logger = logging.getLogger("cupmb")
if not logger.handlers:
    level = os.environ.get("CUPMB_LOGLEVEL", "WARNING").strip().upper()
    if level.isdigit():
        level = int(level)

    stream = logging.StreamHandler()
    stream.setFormatter(ColoredFormatter(_FORMAT))
    stream.setLevel(level)

    logger.propagate = False
    logger.addHandler(stream)
    logger.setLevel(level)


@functools.lru_cache(None)
def log_once(category, msg, logger=logger):
    getattr(logger, category)(msg)
```

### cupmb/utils/nvrtc_build.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#
import os, sys, functools, argparse

try:
    # cuda-python >= 12.6 (cuda-bindings)
    from cuda.bindings import driver as cuda, runtime as cudart, nvrtc
except ImportError:
    # legacy cuda-python layout
    from cuda import cuda, cudart, nvrtc

eprint = functools.partial(print, file=sys.stderr)


def cuda_get_error_enum(error):
    if isinstance(error, cuda.CUresult):
        err, name = cuda.cuGetErrorName(error)
        return name if err == cuda.CUresult.CUDA_SUCCESS else "<unknown>"
    elif isinstance(error, cudart.cudaError_t):
        return cudart.cudaGetErrorName(error)[1]
    elif isinstance(error, nvrtc.nvrtcResult):
        return nvrtc.nvrtcGetErrorString(error)[1]
    else:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Unknown error type: {error}")


def check_cuda_error(result):
    if result[0].value:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "CUDA error code={} ({})".format(result[0].value, cuda_get_error_enum(result[0]).decode("utf-8"))
        )
    if len(result) == 1:
        return None
    elif len(result) == 2:
        return result[1]
    else:
        return result[1:]


CCE = check_cuda_error


def _extract_cuda_version(v):
    return f"{v // 1000}.{(v % 1000) // 10}.{v%10}"


def _encode_arg(a):
    return a.encode("ascii")


def _decode_arg(a):
    return a.decode("ascii")


def _read_lines(filename):
    with open(filename) as f:
        return tuple(filter(len, f.read().split(os.linesep)))


def _determine_target_architecture(major, minor):
    """
    Determine the target architecture and whether we can generate a cubin.

    A cubin is generated when NVRTC >= 11.1 and the device architecture is supported by NVRTC.
    Otherwise PTX is generated for the highest supported virtual architecture not greater than the
    device one, so that the driver can JIT compile it.
    """
    nvrtc_major, nvrtc_minor = CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcVersion())
    use_cubin = (nvrtc_major, nvrtc_minor) >= (11, 1)

    cc = 10 * major + minor
    try:
        supported_archs = tuple(map(int, CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcGetSupportedArchs())))
    except (AttributeError, RuntimeError):
        # nvrtcGetSupportedArchs is only available since NVRTC 11.2
        supported_archs = ()

    if supported_archs and (cc not in supported_archs):
        candidates = tuple(a for a in supported_archs if a <= cc)
        if not candidates:
            msg = (
                f"Device compute capability {major}.{minor} is not supported by NVRTC "
                f"{nvrtc_major}.{nvrtc_minor} (supported architectures are {supported_archs})."
            )
            raise RuntimeError(msg)
        eprint(
            f"Warning: device compute capability {major}.{minor} is not supported by NVRTC "
            f"{nvrtc_major}.{nvrtc_minor}, falling back to PTX for compute_{max(candidates)}."
        )
        cc = max(candidates)
        use_cubin = False

    return cc, use_cubin


def main(args):

    # Check Python-Cuda driver and cuda version
    int_driver_version = CCE(cudart.cudaDriverGetVersion())
    int_cuda_version = CCE(cudart.cudaRuntimeGetVersion())
    driver_version = _extract_cuda_version(int_driver_version)
    cuda_version = _extract_cuda_version(int_cuda_version)

    if int_driver_version < 11000:
        msg = (
            "You are using an outdated NVIDIA driver, expected a CUDA >= 11.0 compatible driver but found version "
            f"{driver_version} ({int_driver_version}). Please upgrade your NVidia driver to the latest stable "
            "driver supported by your device at https://www.nvidia.fr/Download/index.aspx."
        )
        eprint(msg)
        return 1

    if int_cuda_version < 11000:
        msg = (
            "You are using an outdated CUDA version, expected at least CUDA 11.0 but found version "
            f"{cuda_version} ({int_cuda_version}). Please download and install lastest CUDA release from "
            "https://developer.nvidia.com/cuda-downloads and rebuild Python-Cuda."
        )
        eprint(msg)
        return 1

    major = args.major
    minor = args.minor

    try:
        cc, use_cubin = _determine_target_architecture(major, minor)
    except RuntimeError as e:
        eprint(e)
        return 1

    prefix = "sm" if use_cubin else "compute"
    arch_arg = f"--gpu-architecture={prefix}_{cc}"

    build_options = args.build_options
    build_options += (arch_arg,)

    with open(args.source_file) as f:
        code = f.read()

    prog = CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcCreateProgram(_encode_arg(code), _encode_arg(args.source_file), 0, [], []))
    for kname in map(_encode_arg, args.kernel_names):
        CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcAddNameExpression(prog, kname))

    try:
        CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcCompileProgram(prog, len(build_options), list(map(_encode_arg, build_options))))
    except RuntimeError as e:
        log_size = CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcGetProgramLogSize(prog))
        log = b" " * log_size
        CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcGetProgramLog(prog, log))
        log = log.rstrip(b"\x00 ")
        eprint(f"Failed to compile source file {args.source_file}, build options were {build_options}.")
        eprint(_decode_arg(log))
        eprint(e)
        return 1

    mangled_kernel_names = []
    for kname in map(_encode_arg, args.kernel_names):
        mangled_name = CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcGetLoweredName(prog, kname))
        mangled_kernel_names.append(_decode_arg(mangled_name))

    filename = args.output_knames
    with open(filename, "w") as f:
        for mname in mangled_kernel_names:
            f.write(mname)
            f.write(os.linesep)

    if use_cubin:
        dataSize = CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcGetCUBINSize(prog))
        data = b" " * dataSize
        CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcGetCUBIN(prog, data))
    else:
        dataSize = CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcGetPTXSize(prog))
        data = b" " * dataSize
        CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcGetPTX(prog, data))

    filename = args.output_binary
    with open(filename, "wb") as f:
        f.write(data)

    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1:] == ["--nvrtc-version"]:
        # used as a compiler version for the on disk kernel cache
        print("{}.{}".format(*CCE(nvrtc.nvrtcVersion())))
        sys.exit(0)

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="A small utility to build CUPMB kernels "
        "when underlying CUDA backend does not support template __global__ functions."
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--major",
        dest="major",
        type=int,
        default=None,
        help="Device major compute capability.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--minor",
        dest="minor",
        type=int,
        default=None,
        help="Device minor compute capability.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--source-file",
        dest="source_file",
        type=str,
        default=None,
        help="Input CUDA source file.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--input-options",
        dest="input_options",
        type=str,
        default=None,
        help="Source file that defines build options (one per line)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--input-knames",
        dest="input_knames",
        type=str,
        default=None,
        help="Source file that defines input kernel names (one per line).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-knames",
        dest="output_knames",
        type=str,
        default=None,
        help="Target file that will contain mangled kernel names (one per line).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-binary",
        dest="output_binary",
        type=str,
        default=None,
        help="Target binary file that will contain the binary kernels.",
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    assert (args.major is not None) and args.major >= 1, args.major
    assert (args.minor is not None) and args.minor >= 0, args.minor
    assert args.source_file is not None, args.source_file
    assert args.input_options is not None, args.input_options
    assert args.input_knames is not None, args.input_knames
    assert args.output_knames is not None, args.output_knames
    assert args.output_binary is not None, args.output_binary

    if not os.path.isfile(args.source_file):
        msg = f"Source file:///{args.source_file} does not exist."
        parser.error(msg)
    args.source_file = os.path.realpath(args.source_file)

    if not os.path.isfile(args.input_options):
        msg = f"Source file:///{args.input_options} does not exist."
        parser.error(msg)
    args.input_options = os.path.realpath(args.input_options)

    if not os.path.isfile(args.input_knames):
        msg = f"Source file:///{args.input_knames} does not exist."
        parser.error(msg)
    args.input_knames = os.path.realpath(args.input_knames)

    args.kernel_names = _read_lines(args.input_knames)
    args.build_options = _read_lines(args.input_options)

    sys.exit(main(args))
```

### cupmb/utils/types.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#

import numpy as np

dtype_to_ctype = {
    np.bool_: "bool",
    np.int8: "char",
    np.int16: "short",
    np.int32: "int",
    np.int64: "long long",
    np.uint8: "unsigned char",
    np.uint16: "unsigned short",
    np.uint32: "unsigned int",
    np.uint64: "unsigned long long",
    np.float16: "half",
    np.float32: "float",
    np.float64: "double",
    np.complex64: "complex<float>",
    np.complex128: "complex<double>",
}

ctype_to_dtype = {v: k for (k, v) in dtype_to_ctype.items()}
ctype_to_dtype.update(
    {
        "signed char": np.int8,
        "long": np.int64,
        "unsigned long": np.uint64,
        "size_t": np.uint64,
        "int8_t": np.int8,
        "int16_t": np.int16,
        "int32_t": np.int32,
        "int64_t": np.int64,
        "uint8_t": np.uint8,
        "uint16_t": np.uint16,
        "uint32_t": np.uint32,
        "uint64_t": np.uint64,
    }
)


def normalize_scalar_type(t):
    """
    Convert a dtype-like (numpy scalar type, np.dtype instance, numpy type string such as 'float32'
    or C type string such as 'float' or 'unsigned int') to a numpy scalar type.
    """
    if isinstance(t, str) and t.strip() in ctype_to_dtype:
        return ctype_to_dtype[t.strip()]
    return np.dtype(t).type
```

### cupmb/utils/units.py
```
#
# Copyright (c) 2021-2026 Jean-Baptiste Keck <jbkeck@hotmail.com>
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
#

import numpy as np

Number = int | float | np.number


def binary_unit2str(b: Number, unit: str, fmt: str = "{:.1f}") -> str:
    """Convert any unit to a pretty string in binary base."""
    try:
        bb = int(b)
        prefix = ["", "ki", "Mi", "Gi", "Ti", "Pi", "Ei", "Zi", "Yi"]
        i = 0
        while bb >= 1024:
            bb >>= 10
            i += 1
        fmt = fmt if prefix[i] else "{:d}"
        return (fmt + "{}{}").format(b / (1024**i), prefix[i], unit)
    except:
        return (fmt + "{}").format(b, unit)


def decimal_unit2str(b: Number, unit: str, fmt: str = "{:.1f}") -> str:
    """Convert any unit to a pretty string in decimal base."""
    try:
        if b == 0:
            fmt = "{:d}"
            return fmt.format(0) + unit
        elif abs(b) >= 1:  # type: ignore
            bb = int(abs(b))  # type: ignore
            prefix = ["", "k", "M", "G", "T", "P", "E", "Z", "Y"]
            i = 0
            while bb >= 1000:
                bb /= 1000
                i += 1
            fmt = fmt if prefix[i] else "{:d}"
            return (fmt + "{}{}").format(b / (10.0 ** (3 * i)), prefix[i], unit)
        else:
            prefix = ["", "m", "u", "n", "p", "f", "a", "z", "y"]
            i = 0
            bb = b
            while abs(bb) < 1:
                bb *= 1000
                i += 1
            fmt = fmt if prefix[i] else "{:d}"
            return (fmt + "{}{}").format(b * (10.0 ** (3 * i)), prefix[i], unit)
    except:
        return (fmt + "{}").format(b, unit)


def unit2str(b: Number | None, unit: str, decimal: bool, fmt: str = "{:2.1f}") -> str:
    """Convert any unit to a pretty string in binary or decimal base."""

    if not isinstance(b, (int, float, np.number)):
        return str(b)

    if decimal:
        return decimal_unit2str(b, unit, fmt)
    else:
        return binary_unit2str(b, unit, fmt)


def bytes2str(b: Number | None, decimal: bool = False, fmt: str = "{:2.1f}") -> str:
    """Convert input bytes to a pretty string."""
    return unit2str(b, "B", decimal, fmt)


def bdw2str(bdw: Number | None, decimal: bool = True, fmt: str = "{:2.1f}") -> str:
    """Convert an input memory bandwidth in bytes per second to a pretty string."""
    return unit2str(bdw, "B/s", decimal, fmt)


def time2str(t: Number | None, on_zero: str | None = None, on_none: str | None = None) -> str:
    """Convert an input time in seconds to a pretty string."""
    if not isinstance(t, (float, int, np.dtype)):
        return str(t)
    if t is None:
        return f"{-1:5d}" if (on_none is None) else on_none
    elif t < 1e-9:
        return f"{0:5d}" if (on_zero is None) else on_zero
    elif t < 1e-6:
        return f"{t * 1e9:>5.1f}ns"
    elif t < 1e-3:
        return f"{t * 1e6:>5.1f}us"
    elif t < 1e1:
        return f"{t * 1e3:>5.1f}ms"
    else:
        return f"{t:>6.1f}s"
```
